> AGENT

Yes, it makes sense — you're collapsing the level into the terminal: every question is two clicks, **pick the lens, then pick the answer**. No walking, no typing, no keywords. And the tool-choice beat is doing real teaching, because "which of these would actually answer this question" is the genuine skill. Here's the flow written out straight:

## Revised flow

**1. Setup.** Rescue ship overhead, won't dock until the repairs check out. You stand at the ship console — the whole level happens at this one terminal. Clock starts.

**2. Question 1 appears:**

> *"The antenna repair — how many attempts did it take?"*

**3. The terminal offers three ways to find out** (click or press 1/2/3):

> **[1]** `entire checkpoint explain f25c8b30d971`
> **[2]** Skill: *what-happened* — "ask in plain language"
> **[3]** `entire dispatch` — the day's summary

**4. It runs, visibly, and answers in that tool's voice:**

- Pick **[1]** → the full record card prints, real-command style: *"dish unsalvageable — respun the mast into a spire · 3 attempts · the first two fell over."* The answer's in there; you read for it.
- Pick **[2]** → the skill runs the command *for* you — you watch `> entire checkpoint explain f25c8b...` scroll by — then hands you one plain sentence: *"The subagent rebuilt the antenna as a spire; it took 3 attempts."* Same truth, less reading.
- Pick **[3]** → dispatch prints the day summary… which doesn't contain attempt counts. *"That didn't answer it — try another."* A few seconds gone, no other penalty.

**5. Answer chips appear:**

> **[ 2 ]  [ 3 ]  [ 5 ]**

Click **3** → out on the island, the **Signal Spire flares** — *"CERTIFIED, 1/5."* (The world stays in the game even though you don't walk: each right answer lights up the thing it was about.)

**6. Repeat ×5.** Each question's tool menu shifts slightly — a *where* question makes `search "hull plate"` the star, a *what-order* question makes `list` the star — so over five questions you've seen every command answer the kind of question it's for.

**7. Last certification** → rescue ship descends → win screen: *"Every question had an answer in the record."* + the skills install line.

**Fail:** 0:00 before 5/5 → ship leaves → R.

---

Two design decisions inside this, which are really *the* decisions:

**Are tool picks right/wrong, or all-roads-lead-there?** I'd do **mostly all-valid, occasionally dead-end**: usually 2 of 3 options genuinely answer it in different voices (raw command = full record, skill = one friendly sentence — that contrast IS the skills pitch, demonstrated not explained), and 1 is a polite dead end that costs clock. Never a fail, never a lecture.

**What did we trade away?** The walking — and with it most of the 3D-game feel. L1 and L2 are legs-and-world games; this version is a terminal quiz with a pretty backdrop. The flare-on-certify keeps the island involved cheaply, but it's worth saying plainly: this is the least "game-bodied" level of the three. That might be exactly right for a finale (the player graduates from running around to commanding from the chair — fits the operator→delegator arc), or it might feel flat after the drone-bay spectacle. I'd honestly playtest both: this version, and the same beats with one short walk per question. Cheap to compare since everything else is identical.

If you want one more simplification lever: drop the tool menu on questions 1–2 (auto-run, learn the rhythm), introduce the choice on 3–5 once the player knows what answers look like.

> DEVELOPER

it doesnt always have to be skill what happened..explain and dispatch..do you agree

> AGENT

Agree completely — and it's not just variety for its own sake. If the menu is the same three options every time, players stop reading and click the same chip on autopilot, and the one bit of thinking the level has evaporates. Rotating the menu per question is what forces the player to actually read the question and ask "what *kind* of question is this?" — which is the entire lesson.

The principle I'd use: **the question type picks the cast.** Each menu is the right tool, a different-but-also-works tool, and one plausible dead end. Something like:

| Question | Menu might be |
|---|---|
| *"How many attempts did the antenna take?"* (what happened at one site) | `explain` · Skill: *what-happened* · `dispatch` (dead end — too zoomed out) |
| *"Which system used salvaged hull plate?"* (you don't know where) | `search "hull plate"` · Skill: *recall* · `explain` (dead end — explain needs to know *which* checkpoint, that's the problem) |
| *"What got repaired first?"* (order/time) | `list` · `activity` · `search` (dead end — search finds topics, not timelines) |
| *"What would you tell the next crew about the grav skids?"* (handoff flavor) | Skill: *session-handoff* or *teach* · `explain` · `list` (dead end) |
| *"Summarize the repairs for our log."* (finale) | `dispatch` — maybe no menu at all, the ceremonial closer like always |

And per your own rule — we don't have to use every skill. I'd cap the whole level at maybe **2–3 skills and 3–4 commands total**, each appearing once or twice where it's naturally the answer. Eleven skills paraded through five questions would turn it into a product tour, which is the complexity you keep (rightly) killing.

One nice side effect of rotation: the dead ends do quiet teaching too. Picking `explain` when you don't know *which* checkpoint, and watching it fail for exactly that reason, teaches the search-vs-explain boundary better than any tooltip — and it only costs a few clock seconds.

The one thing I'd keep constant across all five questions is the *shape* — question → 3 tools → output → 3 answers → flare — so the rhythm stays jump-collect-shoot simple while the contents rotate.

> DEVELOPER

and i like the most all valid..occassional dead end

> AGENT

Good call — and it fits everything you've been pushing this design toward. "All valid" means the player can't really lose at the tool menu, so there's no quiz anxiety and no "what keyword were they looking for" frustration — just different lenses on the same truth, which is the honest pitch anyway (a skill and a raw command really do get you the same answer in different voices). The occasional dead end keeps the menu from being decorative — choices only feel like choices if one of them can fizzle — and the fizzle itself teaches the tool's boundary for the price of a few clock seconds. Failure stays where it belongs: the level clock, same as L1 and L2.

Let me capture the converged design in memory before it only lives in this conversation.

Saved to my notes. Here's where the Level 3 design now stands after this session:

**Locked (your calls today):**
- Final level, whole thing at one console — no walking; the island participates by flaring each certified upgrade.
- Per question: plain-words question → ~3 tools as multiple choice → output in that tool's voice → answer chips → flare. Two clicks, zero typing, zero keyword guessing.
- Tool menus **rotate by question type** and are **mostly all-valid with an occasional dead end** that costs only clock time.
- Small cast — a few skills, a few commands, not the whole catalog.
- The aha: questions you provably can't answer from memory, answered from your own L1/L2 record.

**Still open for you to react to later:** the rescue-ship fiction vs. something plainer, question count (~5), whether questions 1–2 auto-run the tool before the menu appears, whether the "discrepancy" question (grav skids, "torque spec: vibes") makes the cut, and the terminal-quiz-vs-one-short-walk playtest question.

When you're ready for it to be durable beyond my memory notes, I can write this into PLAN.md as "Level 3 design (PROPOSED — converged 2026-06-10, not built)" in the same format as the L1/L2 sections — just say so. No code until you give the word.

> DEVELOPER

why the rescue ship if i've already fixed my ship..maybe it's like we're inside the ship and we need to confirm that we can take off..like this part of the pass code or something like that..

> AGENT

The rescue ship was a plot hole, you're right — you spent two levels fixing *your* ship; someone else's ship swooping in to save you undercuts the whole game. Your version is better, and it closes the story in a way the rescue ship never could:

**The launch sequence framing.** You're inside your own ship, in the pilot's chair — first time the game has put you there, which alone signals "finale." The ship is physically repaired, but the launch computer won't arm until the work is verified: *it won't fly on work nobody can account for* — which is literally the rule L2 already established for powering systems, so the fiction isn't even new, it's the same rule escalated to the highest stakes. Pre-flight check, but the checklist questions can only be answered from the record.

And there's a story rhyme sitting right there that I'd absolutely use: **the ship's AI is the amnesiac from Level 1.** It lost its memory in the crash; you spent the game rebuilding that memory out of checkpoints. Now, at launch, it's the one asking the questions — and every answer comes out of the memory *you* banked for it. The game's first scene and last scene are the same character, before and after. That's the arc: L1 you gave the ship its memory back, L3 the memory proves it can carry you home.

The **passcode idea maps perfectly onto the chips** with zero mechanical change: each answer chip you pick isn't just "correct" — it slots into a launch code segment on the console (`▣ ▣ □ □ □`... filling up). "3 attempts" → a segment locks, a system arms, something on the dash goes green and you hear it. Five segments → code complete → **countdown → liftoff** → the actual ending of the game, the thing the stranded-astronaut premise promised on day one. Win screen plays over the planet falling away below — your labeled upgrades shrinking on the island — with the skills line at the bottom.

Everything else survives untouched: same question → rotating tool menu (mostly all-valid, occasional dead end) → output → answer chips loop; the flare-on-certify just becomes a console segment locking + maybe the corresponding system humming to life around you in the cabin. Fail state even gets better: clock at 0:00 isn't "the ship leaves," it's "launch window missed" — same window pressure L2 already used.

One small question to sit with (not answer now): does the player need to know *why* the ship asks instead of checking its own records? I'd keep it to one line in the briefing — something like "I can read the record. Protocol says you have to be the one to confirm it." — which is also, quietly, the truest line in the game about what humans are for in agentic workflows.

```
29	    const playing = !bgm.paused;
30	    const awaitingStart = !playing && !userPausedMusic;
31	    audioPanel.classList.toggle("is-awaiting-start", awaitingStart);
32	    audioToggle.classList.toggle("is-playing", playing);
33	    audioToggle.setAttribute("aria-label", playing ? "Pause music" : "Start music");
34	    audioToggle.title = playing ? "Pause music" : "Start music";
35	    audioMute.classList.toggle("is-muted", bgm.muted || bgm.volume === 0);
36	    audioMute.setAttribute("aria-label", bgm.muted ? "Unmute music" : "Mute music");
37	    audioMute.title = bgm.muted ? "Unmute music" : "Mute music";
38	  };
39	
40	  const tryPlayMusic = () => {
41	    if (userPausedMusic) return;
42	    bgm.play().then(updateAudioUi).catch(updateAudioUi);
43	  };
44	  const removeGestureMusicStart = () => {
45	    for (const eventName of ["pointerdown", "click", "keydown", "touchstart"]) {
46	      window.removeEventListener(eventName, startMusicOnGesture, true);
47	    }
48	  };
49	  const startMusicOnGesture = (event) => {
50	    if (event.target?.closest?.("#audio-panel")) return;
51	    tryPlayMusic();
52	  };
53	
54	  audioToggle.addEventListener("click", () => {
55	    if (bgm.paused) {
56	      userPausedMusic = false;
57	      tryPlayMusic();
58	    } else {
59	      userPausedMusic = true;
60	      bgm.pause();
61	      updateAudioUi();
62	    }
63	  });
64	
65	  audioMute.addEventListener("click", () => {
66	    bgm.muted = !bgm.muted;
67	    updateAudioUi();
68	  });
69	
70	  audioVolume.addEventListener("input", () => {
71	    bgm.volume = Number(audioVolume.value);
72	    if (bgm.volume > 0 && bgm.muted) bgm.muted = false;
73	    updateAudioUi();
74	  });
75	
76	  bgm.addEventListener("play", () => {
77	    removeGestureMusicStart();
78	    updateAudioUi();
79	  });
80	  bgm.addEventListener("pause", updateAudioUi);
81	  bgm.addEventListener("volumechange", updateAudioUi);
82	
83	  for (const eventName of ["pointerdown", "click", "keydown", "touchstart"]) {
84	    window.addEventListener(eventName, startMusicOnGesture, true);
85	  }
86	
87	  tryPlayMusic();
88	  updateAudioUi();
89	}
90	
91	const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
92	const requestedView = params.get("view");
93	const jumpToLevel2 = requestedView === "level2" || params.get("level") === "2";
94	
95	// ---------- views ----------
96	// After Level 1's `entire checkpoint list`, the ship wakes its drone bay:
97	// the orbit pin glitches and landing again enters Level 2 ("The Drone Bay").
98	// The shelved search level ("The Archive") stays reachable at ?view=archive.
99	let level1Done = jumpToLevel2;
100	
101	const planetView = createPlanetView(renderer, {
102	  onIslandClick: () => switchTo(level1Done ? droneBayView : islandView),
103	});
104	const islandView = createIslandView(renderer, {
105	  onExit: () => switchTo(planetView),
106	  onComplete: () => { level1Done = true; },
107	});
108	const droneBayView = createDroneBayView(renderer, {
109	  onExit: () => switchTo(planetView),
110	});
111	const archiveView = createArchiveView(renderer, {
112	  onExit: () => switchTo(planetView),
113	});
114	
115	// Default to the orbit view; ?view=island jumps straight to Level 1,
116	// ?view=level2 (or ?level=2) to Level 2, ?view=archive to the shelved
117	// search level — handy for development.
118	let current = requestedView === "island" ? islandView
119	  : requestedView === "archive" ? archiveView
120	  : jumpToLevel2 ? droneBayView : planetView;
121	current.enter();
122	
123	// ---------- fade transition ----------
124	const fade = document.getElementById("fade");
125	const hint = document.getElementById("hint");
126	const pin = document.getElementById("island-pin");
127	let transitioning = false;
128	
129	function refreshOrbitHud() {
130	  hint.classList.toggle("hidden", current !== planetView);
131	  hint.textContent = level1Done
132	    ? "A new signal — the ship's drone bay just woke up. Click the pin to land"
133	    : "Drag to orbit · Scroll to zoom · Click the pin to land";
134	  pin?.classList.toggle("is-corrupted", level1Done);
135	}
136	refreshOrbitHud();
137	
138	function switchTo(view) {
139	  if (transitioning || view === current) return;
140	  transitioning = true;
141	  fade.classList.add("show");
142	  setTimeout(() => {
143	    current.exit();
144	    current = view;
145	    current.enter();
146	    refreshOrbitHud();
147	    fade.classList.remove("show");
148	    transitioning = false;
149	  }, 650); // match #fade CSS transition
150	}
151	
152	// ---------- resize ----------
153	window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
154	  renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
155	  planetView.resize();
156	  islandView.resize();
157	  droneBayView.resize();
158	  archiveView.resize();
159	});
160	
161	// ---------- loop ----------
162	const clock = new THREE.Clock();
163	let firstFrame = true;
164	
165	function animate() {
166	  const dt = Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.05); // clamp big tab-switch jumps
167	  const t = clock.elapsedTime;
168	  current.update(dt, t);
169	  renderer.render(current.scene, current.camera);
170	
171	  if (firstFrame) {
172	    firstFrame = false;
173	    document.getElementById("loader").classList.add("done");
174	  }
175	  requestAnimationFrame(animate);
176	}
177	animate();
178
```

```
1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	
3	// Shared props for the memory levels: the findable light beam, the ice block a
4	// commit freezes a memory into, and the floating `Entire-Checkpoint: <id>`
5	// trailer sprite. Used by Level 1 (islandView) and Level 2 (archiveView).
6	
7	export function makeBeamTexture() {
8	  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
9	  c.width = 8; c.height = 128;
10	  const ctx = c.getContext("2d");
11	  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 128);
12	  g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,210,122,0)");
13	  g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(255,210,122,0.9)");
14	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
15	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 128);
16	  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
17	  t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
18	  return t;
19	}
20	
21	// Translucent crystal that encases a memory once it's committed.
22	export function makeIceBlock() {
23	  const geo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(3.4, 0);
24	  const mat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
25	    color: 0xbfe9ff,
26	    metalness: 0,
27	    roughness: 0.06,
28	    transmission: 0.5,
29	    thickness: 2.6,
30	    ior: 1.3,
31	    transparent: true,
32	    opacity: 0.62,
33	    emissive: 0x0a2230,
34	    emissiveIntensity: 0.35,
35	    flatShading: true,
36	  });
37	  return new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
38	}
39	
40	// Floating label above a frozen block — the commit trailer Entire stamps on:
41	//   "Entire-Checkpoint: <12-char hash>" (dim key + bright hash).
42	export function makeIdSprite(id) {
43	  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
44	  c.width = 768; c.height = 64;
45	  const ctx = c.getContext("2d");
46	  ctx.font = "bold 28px ui-monospace, monospace";
47	  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
48	  ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(111,227,255,0.9)";
49	  ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
50	
51	  const key = "Entire-Checkpoint: ";
52	  const keyW = ctx.measureText(key).width;
53	  const idW = ctx.measureText(id).width;
54	  let x = (c.width - (keyW + idW)) / 2;       // center the whole trailer
55	  ctx.textAlign = "left";
56	  ctx.fillStyle = "#8fc4dc";                    // dim trailer key
57	  ctx.fillText(key, x, 34);
58	  ctx.fillStyle = "#dff3ff";                    // bright hash
59	  ctx.fillText(id, x + keyW, 34);
60	
61	  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
62	  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
63	  const spr = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({
64	    map: tex, transparent: true, depthWrite: false, fog: false,
65	  }));
66	  spr.scale.set(30, 2.5, 1);                    // 12:1 canvas → keep text height
67	  return spr;
68	}
69	
70	// Entire checkpoints get a 12-character hex id (e.g. 711044b1fe29).
71	export function genCheckpointId() {
72	  const hex = "0123456789abcdef";
73	  let s = "";
74	  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) s += hex[Math.floor(Math.random() * 16)];
75	  return s;
76	}
77
```

```
7	// LEVEL 2 ("The Drone Bay") — the same island, revisited.
8	//
9	// The day-to-day agentic loop, played: delegate → review → accept.
10	// Five ship systems are dark; five drones can fix them in parallel while you
11	// can only be in one place. One new command on top of what L1 taught:
12	//
13	//   walk to a dark system, press E      → a drone takes the job, you're free
14	//   the drone finishes while you're away → its work seals under a checkpoint
15	//   walk up → `entire checkpoint explain <id>` is PRE-FILLED — press Enter
16	//   read what it actually did            → ADD TO SHIP (Y) → system online
17	//   the FIFTH accept keeps the terminal open: type `entire dispatch` right
18	//   there — the day's report writes itself from the checkpoints. Finish line.
19	//
20	// Drones don't repair — they IMPROVISE (a bent dish comes back as a signal
21	// spire). The transformation is what makes you NEED the explanation.

206	function buildStarDome() {
207	  const g = new THREE.Group();

211	  const pts = new Float32Array(120 * 3);
212	  for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {
213	    // random points in a dome above the pedestal
214	    const r = 1.2 + Math.random() * 1.4;
215	    const th = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
216	    const ph = Math.random() * Math.PI * 0.5;
217	    pts[i * 3] = Math.cos(th) * Math.sin(ph) * r;
218	    pts[i * 3 + 1] = 3.2 + Math.cos(ph) * r * 0.9;
219	    pts[i * 3 + 2] = Math.sin(th) * Math.sin(ph) * r;
220	  }
221	  stars.setAttribute("position", new THREE.BufferAttribute(pts, 3));
222	  const field = new THREE.Points(stars, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
223	    color: 0xbfe9ff, size: 0.16, transparent: true, opacity: 0.95,
224	    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false,

276	  domeGlass.position.y = 0.6;
277	  g.add(tray, domeGlass);
278	  g.userData.anim = { type: "pod" };
279	  return g;
280	}
281	function buildGravSkid() {
282	  const g = new THREE.Group();
283	  const pad = new THREE.Mesh(
284	    new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.7, 1.9, 0.5, 12),
285	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x2c3140, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.35, emissive: 0x123a4a, emissiveIntensity: 0.7 })
286	  );
287	  pad.position.y = 1.6;
288	  const glow = new THREE.Mesh(
289	    new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.5, 0.12, 8, 32),
290	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.65 })
291	  );
292	  glow.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
293	  glow.position.y = 0.5;
294	  g.add(pad, glow);
295	  g.userData.anim = { type: "skid", pad, glow };
296	  return g;
297	}
298	
299	// ---------- the little worker drone ----------
300	function buildDrone() {
301	  const g = new THREE.Group();
302	  const body = new THREE.Mesh(
303	    new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.7, 0),
304	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x2c3140, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.3, emissive: 0x2a8aa6, emissiveIntensity: 1.0 })
305	  );
306	  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
307	    new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.0, 0.09, 8, 24),
308	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.8 })
309	  );
310	  ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
311	  g.add(body, ring);
312	  g.userData = { body, ring };
313	  return g;
314	}
315	
316	// The ship console the terminal lives in.
317	function makeConsole() {
318	  const g = new THREE.Group();
319	  const pedestal = new THREE.Mesh(
320	    new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6, 4.6, 1.6),
321	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1c2230, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.35 })
322	  );
323	  pedestal.position.y = 2.3;
324	  const screen = new THREE.Mesh(
325	    new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.1, 1.3),
326	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
327	      color: 0x0c2030, emissive: 0x2a8aa6, emissiveIntensity: 1.4, roughness: 0.3,
328	    })
329	  );
330	  screen.position.set(0, 4.0, 0.82);
331	  screen.rotation.x = -0.28;
332	  g.add(pedestal, screen);
333	  return g;
334	}
335	
336	export function createDroneBayView(renderer, { onExit } = {}) {
337	  const canvas = renderer.domElement;
338	
339	  // ---------- scene & sky ----------
340	  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
341	  scene.background = SKY_CALM.clone();
342	  scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(SKY_CALM.clone(), 90, 320);
343	
344	  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(
345	    62, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 2000
346	  );
347	
348	  // ---------- lighting ----------
349	  scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xcdbcff, 0x3a2f5e, 0.7));
350	  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(SUN_CALM, 1.5);
351	  sun.position.set(60, 90, 40);
352	  scene.add(sun);
353	  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x6a5a92, 0.3));
354	
355	  const dome = new THREE.Mesh(
356	    new THREE.SphereGeometry(900, 32, 16),
357	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: DOME_CALM.clone(), side: THREE.BackSide, fog: false })
358	  );
359	  scene.add(dome);
360	
361	  // ---------- terrain (the same island) ----------
362	  const terrain = createTerrain({ size: 200, segments: 220, maxHeight: 26, seed: 1337 });
363	  scene.add(terrain.mesh, terrain.water);
364	
365	  const beamTex = makeBeamTexture();
366	
367	  // ---------- the five work sites ----------
368	  const sites = SYSTEMS.map((sys, idx) => {
369	    const anchor = new THREE.Group();
370	    anchor.position.set(sys.pos.x, terrain.heightAt(sys.pos.x, sys.pos.z), sys.pos.z);
371	
372	    const BUILD = {
373	      buildBrokenCoils, buildBrokenNav, buildBrokenDish, buildBrokenVent, buildBrokenStrut,
374	      buildPlasmaRing, buildStarDome, buildGardenPod, buildGravSkid,
375	      buildSignalSpire: () => buildSignalSpire(beamTex),
376	    };
377	    const brokenModel = BUILD[sys.broken]();
378	    const upgradeModel = BUILD[sys.upgrade]();
379	    upgradeModel.visible = false;
380	    anchor.add(brokenModel, upgradeModel);
381	
382	    // Status beam — readable from across the island:
383	    // amber = broken, cyan pulse = drone at work, ice-blue = sealed (review me).
384	    const beam = new THREE.Mesh(
385	      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.6, 0.6, 60, 12, 1, true),
386	      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
387	        map: beamTex, color: 0xff8a5c, transparent: true, opacity: 0.3,
388	        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide,
389	        fog: false,
390	      })
391	    );
392	    beam.position.y = 30;
393	    anchor.add(beam);
394	
395	    // The checkpoint seal: ice + floating trailer id, like Level 1's bank.
396	    const ice = makeIceBlock();
397	    ice.scale.setScalar(1.25);
398	    ice.position.y = 2.6;
399	    ice.visible = false;
400	    anchor.add(ice);
401	    const sprite = makeIdSprite(sys.ckpt);
402	    sprite.position.y = 8.4;
403	    sprite.visible = false;
404	    anchor.add(sprite);
405	
406	    scene.add(anchor);
407	    return {
408	      sys, idx, anchor, brokenModel, upgradeModel, beam, ice, sprite,
409	      state: "broken",      // broken → working → sealed → added
410	      workT: 0,
411	      meltT: 0,             // ice melt animation after ADD TO SHIP
412	    };
413	  });
414	
415	  // ---------- the drone bay: five parked drones by the console ----------
416	  const drones = SYSTEMS.map((_, i) => {
417	    const d = buildDrone();
418	    const x = BAY_ROW.x0 + i * BAY_ROW.dx;
419	    const home = new THREE.Vector3(x, terrain.heightAt(x, BAY_ROW.z) + 1.2, BAY_ROW.z);
420	    d.position.copy(home);
421	    scene.add(d);
422	    return {
423	      model: d, home,
424	      state: "bay",         // bay → flying → working → returning
425	      site: null,
426	      from: new THREE.Vector3(), to: new THREE.Vector3(),
427	      t: 0, dur: 1,
428	      bobOff: Math.random() * 10,
429	    };
430	  });
431	
432	  // Work beam shown under a working drone (one per drone, toggled).
433	  const workBeams = drones.map(() => {
434	    const m = new THREE.Mesh(
435	      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.25, 1.2, 6, 10, 1, true),
436	      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
437	        color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.5,
438	        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide,
439	      })
440	    );
441	    m.visible = false;
442	    scene.add(m);
443	    return m;
444	  });
445	
446	  // ---------- the ship console ----------
447	  const consoleAnchor = new THREE.Group();
448	  consoleAnchor.position.set(
449	    CONSOLE_POS.x, terrain.heightAt(CONSOLE_POS.x, CONSOLE_POS.z), CONSOLE_POS.z
450	  );
451	  consoleAnchor.add(makeConsole());
452	  const consoleGlow = new THREE.PointLight(0xffd27a, 5, 28, 2);
453	  consoleGlow.position.y = 5;
454	  consoleAnchor.add(consoleGlow);
455	  scene.add(consoleAnchor);
456	
457	  // ---------- first-person controller ----------
458	  const fp = createFirstPerson(camera, canvas, {
459	    heightAt: terrain.heightAt,
460	    radius: terrain.radius * 0.96,
461	    eyeHeight: 2.6,
462	    speed: 24,
463	  });
464	  scene.add(fp.controls.object);
465	
466	  // Bird's-eye map (M) — see all five beams at once, keep walking meanwhile.
467	  const overhead = createOverhead(scene, terrain, camera);
468	  function setMap(on) {
469	    if (overhead.on === on) return;
470	    overhead.set(on);
471	    fp.setAlwaysMove(on);                       // arrows work without pointer lock
472	    scene.fog.near = on ? 500 : 90;             // don't fog the map out
473	    scene.fog.far = on ? 1400 : 320;
474	    crosshair?.classList.toggle("hidden", on || !fp.isLocked);
475	  }
476	
477	  // ---------- HUD elements (shared FP set + this level's own) ----------
478	  const promptEl = document.getElementById("fp-prompt");
479	  const controlsEl = document.getElementById("fp-controls");
480	  const crosshair = document.getElementById("crosshair");
481	  const tutorialEl = document.getElementById("tutorial");
482	  const fpShared = document.getElementById("fp-shared");
483	  const termEl = document.getElementById("terminal");
484	  const termHint = document.getElementById("term-hint");
485	  const termInput = document.getElementById("term-input");
486	  const termList = document.getElementById("term-list");
487	  const termMsg = document.getElementById("term-msg");
488	  const termCta = document.getElementById("term-cta");
489	  const dbHud = document.getElementById("db-hud");
490	  const briefingEl = document.getElementById("db-briefing");
491	  const briefingStartBtn = document.getElementById("db-briefing-start");
492	  const countdownEl = document.getElementById("db-countdown");
493	  const countdownTime = document.getElementById("db-countdown-time");
494	  const systemsEl = document.getElementById("db-systems-rows");
495	  const winEl = document.getElementById("db-win");
496	  const winSub = document.getElementById("db-win-sub");
497	  const failEl = document.getElementById("db-fail");
498	
499	  let active = false;
500	  let started = false;
501	  let failed = false;
502	  let reportSent = false;       // `entire dispatch` has been run — the win
503	  let promptText = null;
504	  let controlsLocked = null;
505	  const taught = new Set();
506	  let tutorialTimer = null;
507	  let msgTimer = null;
508	  let winTimer = null;
509	
510	  // Level countdown — the single source of pressure for the whole run.
511	  let timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
512	  let timerRunning = false;
513	
514	  // Terminal state. Two modes share the one terminal:
515	  //  "review" — pre-filled `entire checkpoint explain <id>` at a sealed site
516	  //  "report" — type `entire dispatch` (the finish line). Opens in place when
517	  //             the FIFTH system is accepted; reopens at any online system or
518	  //             the bay console if the player wandered off first.
519	  let terminalOpen = false;
520	  let termMode = null;
521	  let reviewSite = null;        // the sealed site being reviewed
522	  let explained = false;        // Enter pressed — the card is showing
523	  let buffer = "";
524	  let dismissedSite = null;     // site the player Esc'd out of (until they leave)
525	  let reportDismissed = false;
526	
527	  const addedCount = () => sites.filter((s) => s.state === "added").length;
528	  const allAdded = () => addedCount() >= sites.length;
529	
530	  // ---------- HUD helpers ----------
531	  function setPrompt(text) {
532	    if (!promptEl || text === promptText) return;
533	    promptText = text;
534	    if (text) { promptEl.textContent = text; promptEl.classList.remove("hidden"); }
535	    else promptEl.classList.add("hidden");
536	  }
537	  function showTutorial(text, ms = 5500) {
538	    if (!tutorialEl) return;
539	    clearTimeout(tutorialTimer);
540	    tutorialEl.textContent = text;
541	    tutorialEl.classList.remove("hidden");
542	    if (ms > 0) tutorialTimer = setTimeout(() => tutorialEl.classList.add("hidden"), ms);
543	  }
544	  function teachOnce(key, text, ms) {
545	    if (taught.has(key)) return;
546	    taught.add(key);
547	    showTutorial(text, ms);
548	  }
549	  function setControls(locked) {
550	    if (!controlsEl) return;
551	    if (locked === controlsLocked && !controlsEl.classList.contains("hidden")) return;
552	    controlsLocked = locked;
553	    controlsEl.innerHTML = `
554	      <span class="control-item">
555	        <span class="control-label">To move</span>
556	        <span class="arrow-keys" aria-label="Arrow keys">
557	          <span class="key key-up">↑</span>
558	          <span class="key key-left">←</span>
559	          <span class="key key-down">↓</span>
560	          <span class="key key-right">→</span>
561	        </span>
562	      </span>
563	      ${locked ? `
564	        <span class="control-item">
565	          <span class="control-label">To look around</span>
566	          <span class="mouse-hint">
567	            <span class="mouse-icon" aria-hidden="true"></span>
568	            <span>move mouse</span>
569	          </span>
570	        </span>
571	      ` : ""}
572	      <span class="control-item">
573	        <span class="control-label">Send a subagent</span>
574	        <span class="key">E</span>
575	      </span>
576	      <span class="control-item">
577	        <span class="control-label">Bird's-eye view</span>
578	        <span class="key">M</span>
579	      </span>
580	      <span class="control-item">
581	        <span class="control-label">Return to orbit</span>
582	        <span class="key">B</span>
583	      </span>
584	    `;
585	    controlsEl.classList.remove("hidden");
586	  }
587	  function hideControls() {
588	    controlsLocked = null;
589	    controlsEl?.classList.add("hidden");
590	  }
591	
592	  // ---------- systems status panel ----------
593	  const STATE_LABEL = {
594	    broken: "BROKEN", working: "SUBAGENT AT WORK", sealed: "READY TO REVIEW", added: "ONLINE",
595	  };
596	  function updateSystems() {
597	    if (!systemsEl) return;
598	    systemsEl.innerHTML = sites.map((s) =>
599	      `<div class="db-sys is-${s.state}">` +
600	      `<span class="db-sys-name">${s.sys.name}</span>` +
601	      `<span class="db-sys-state">${STATE_LABEL[s.state]}</span></div>`
602	    ).join("");
603	  }
604	
605	  // ---------- level countdown ----------
606	  function fmtTime(s) {
607	    s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(s));
608	    return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
609	  }
610	  function updateClock() {
611	    if (countdownTime) countdownTime.textContent = fmtTime(timeLeft);
612	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-low", timerRunning && timeLeft <= LOW_TIME);
613	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-critical", timerRunning && timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME);
614	  }
615	  function panicFactor() {
616	    if (failed) return 1;
617	    if (!timerRunning || timeLeft > PANIC_TIME) return 0;
618	    let p = (PANIC_TIME - timeLeft) / PANIC_TIME;
619	    p *= p;
620	    if (timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME) {
621	      const throb = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 85);
622	      p += 0.14 * throb;
623	    }
624	    return Math.min(1, p);
625	  }
626	  function applyPanicSky() {
627	    const p = reportSent ? 0 : panicFactor();
628	    scene.background.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(SKY_PANIC, p);
629	    scene.fog.color.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(FOG_PANIC, p);
630	    dome.material.color.copy(DOME_CALM).lerp(DOME_PANIC, p);
631	    sun.color.copy(SUN_CALM).lerp(SUN_PANIC, p * 0.85);
632	  }
633	
634	  // ---------- briefing / win / fail / reset ----------
635	  function showBriefing() {
636	    timerRunning = false;
637	    fp.detach();
638	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
639	    briefingEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
640	  }
641	  function startLevel() {
642	    if (started) return;
643	    started = true;
644	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
645	    fp.attach();
646	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
647	    timerRunning = true;
648	    updateClock();
649	    showTutorial("Five systems are dark — the amber beams. Walk to one and press E: a subagent takes the job while you move on.", 8500);
650	  }
651	  briefingStartBtn?.addEventListener("click", startLevel);
652	
653	  function failLevel() {
654	    if (failed || reportSent) return;
655	    failed = true;
656	    timerRunning = false;
657	    setMap(false);
658	    closeTerminal(false);
659	    fp.detach();
660	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
661	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
662	    failEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
663	  }
664	  function resetLevel() {
665	    failed = false;
666	    reportSent = false;
667	    dismissedSite = null;
668	    reportDismissed = false;
669	    clearTimeout(winTimer);
670	    for (const s of sites) {
671	      s.state = "broken";
672	      s.workT = 0; s.meltT = 0;
673	      s.brokenModel.visible = true;
674	      s.upgradeModel.visible = false;
675	      const anim = s.upgradeModel.userData.anim;
676	      if (anim?.type === "spire") anim.beam.visible = false;
677	      s.ice.visible = false;
678	      s.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;
679	      s.sprite.visible = false;
680	    }
681	    for (let i = 0; i < drones.length; i++) {
682	      drones[i].state = "bay";
683	      drones[i].site = null;
684	      drones[i].model.position.copy(drones[i].home);
685	      workBeams[i].visible = false;
686	    }
687	    failEl?.classList.add("hidden");
688	    winEl?.classList.add("hidden");
689	    updateSystems();
690	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
691	    timerRunning = true;
692	    updateClock();
693	    if (active) fp.attach();
694	    showTutorial("New attempt — the fleet works in parallel. Use all five subagents.", 6000);
695	  }
696	
697	  // ---------- drone dispatching ----------
698	  function assignDrone(site) {
699	    const drone = drones.find((d) => d.state === "bay");
700	    if (!drone) return;                 // shouldn't happen: one drone per system
701	    site.state = "working";
702	    site.workT = 0;
703	    drone.state = "flying";
704	    drone.site = site;
705	    drone.from.copy(drone.model.position);
706	    drone.to.set(site.anchor.position.x, site.anchor.position.y + 6.5, site.anchor.position.z);
707	    drone.t = 0;
708	    drone.dur = Math.max(1, drone.from.distanceTo(drone.to) / DRONE_SPEED);
709	    updateSystems();
710	    teachOnce("assigned",
711	      "The subagent's got it — you're free. Send the others while it works; the clock won't wait.", 7000);
712	  }
713	  function sealSite(site) {
714	    site.state = "sealed";
715	    site.brokenModel.visible = false;
716	    site.upgradeModel.visible = true;   // built — but dormant until ADD TO SHIP
717	    site.ice.visible = true;
718	    site.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;
719	    site.sprite.visible = true;
720	    updateSystems();
721	    teachOnce("sealed",
722	      "A subagent finished while you were away — its work is sealed under a checkpoint. Walk up and see what it actually did.", 8000);
723	  }
724	
725	  // ---------- terminal ----------
726	  function flashTerminal(text, ok) {
727	    if (!termMsg) return;
728	    clearTimeout(msgTimer);
729	    termMsg.textContent = text;
730	    termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
731	    termMsg.classList.add(ok ? "show-ok" : "show-err");
732	    msgTimer = setTimeout(() => termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err"), 3600);
733	  }
734	  function renderTerminal() {
735	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
736	    if (termMode === "review") {
737	      if (!explained) {
738	        // The command comes pre-filled — you never type a checkpoint id.
739	        termHint.textContent = "# what did the subagent actually do? ask the checkpoint";
740	        termInput.textContent = `entire checkpoint explain ${reviewSite.sys.ckpt}`;
741	        termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
742	        if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
743	          `<span class="cta-label">PRESS</span>` +
744	          `<kbd class="cta-key cta-key-yes">Enter</kbd><span class="cta-note">run it</span>`;
745	      } else {
746	        termHint.textContent = "# reviewed — your call";
747	        termInput.textContent = `entire checkpoint explain ${reviewSite.sys.ckpt}`;
748	        termInput.classList.add("is-dim");
749	        if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
750	          `<button id="db-add-btn" type="button">ADD TO SHIP</button>` +
751	          `<span class="cta-note">or press <kbd class="cta-key cta-key-yes">Y</kbd></span>`;
752	      }
753	    } else if (termMode === "report") {
754	      termHint.textContent = reportSent
755	        ? "# dispatch sent — the day is on the record"
756	        : "# five jobs accepted — send the day's report";
757	      termInput.textContent = buffer;
758	      termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
759	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML = reportSent ? "" :
760	        `<span class="cta-label">TYPE</span><span class="cta-cmd">entire dispatch</span>`;
761	    }
762	  }
763	  function openTerminal(mode, site) {
764	    if (terminalOpen) return;
765	    terminalOpen = true;
766	    termMode = mode;
767	    reviewSite = site || null;
768	    explained = false;
769	    buffer = "";
770	    fp.detach();
771	    termMsg?.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
772	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
773	    termEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
774	    renderTerminal();
775	  }
776	  function closeTerminal(dismiss) {
777	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
778	    if (dismiss) {
779	      if (termMode === "review" && reviewSite) dismissedSite = reviewSite;
780	      if (termMode === "report") reportDismissed = true;
781	    }
782	    terminalOpen = false;
783	    termMode = null;
784	    reviewSite = null;
785	    explained = false;
786	    buffer = "";
787	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
788	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
789	    if (active && !failed) fp.attach();
790	  }
791	
792	  // ---------- entire checkpoint explain (pre-filled, Enter to run) ----------
793	  function runExplain() {
794	    if (!reviewSite || explained) return;
795	    explained = true;
796	    const s = reviewSite;
797	    if (termList) {
798	      termList.innerHTML =
799	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">checkpoint</span>` +
800	        `<span class="tl-title">${s.sys.ckpt}</span></div>` +
801	        s.sys.card.map(([k, v]) =>
802	          `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">${k}</span>` +
803	          `<span class="tl-title">${v}</span></div>`
804	        ).join("");
805	      termList.classList.remove("hidden");
806	    }
807	    flashTerminal(`explained ${s.sys.ckpt} — ${s.sys.name.toLowerCase()} is now a ${s.sys.became}`, true);
808	    teachOnce("explained",
809	      "That's the story behind the checkpoint — work you didn't watch is never a mystery. If it's good, add it to the ship.", 8000);
810	    renderTerminal();
811	  }
812	
813	  // ---------- ADD TO SHIP ----------
814	  function addToShip() {
815	    if (!reviewSite || !explained) return;
816	    const s = reviewSite;
817	    s.state = "added";
818	    s.meltT = MELT_DUR;                 // the seal melts; the upgrade wakes up
819	    const anim = s.upgradeModel.userData.anim;
820	    if (anim?.type === "spire") anim.beam.visible = true;
821	    updateSystems();
822	    if (allAdded()) {
823	      // The fifth accept doesn't close the terminal — the finish line is the
824	      // next command at the prompt you're already at, like the real CLI.
825	      termMode = "report";
826	      reviewSite = null;
827	      explained = false;
828	      buffer = "";
829	      termList?.classList.add("hidden");
830	      flashTerminal("all five systems online", true);
831	      showTutorial("Every system accounted for — send the day's report: type `entire dispatch`.", 0);
832	      renderTerminal();
833	    } else {
834	      closeTerminal(false);
835	      showTutorial(`${s.sys.name} online (${addedCount()} / ${sites.length}).`, 5000);
836	    }
837	  }
838	  // The ADD TO SHIP button (terminal CTA is re-rendered, so delegate the click).
839	  termCta?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
840	    if (!active) return;
841	    if (e.target?.id === "db-add-btn") addToShip();
842	  });
843	
844	  // ---------- the finish line: entire dispatch ----------
845	  // "Generate a dispatch summarizing recent agent work" — five drones just
846	  // worked; the report writes itself from their checkpoints.
847	  function sendDispatch() {
848	    reportSent = true;
849	    timerRunning = false;
850	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
851	    if (termList) {
852	      termList.innerHTML =
853	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">DISPATCH</span>` +
854	        `<span class="tl-title">drone bay — day report</span></div>` +
855	        sites.map((s) =>
856	          `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">·</span>` +
857	          `<span class="tl-title">${s.sys.name.toLowerCase()} → ${s.sys.became} (${s.sys.ckpt.slice(0, 6)}…)</span></div>`
858	        ).join("") +
859	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">filed</span>` +
860	        `<span class="tl-title">from 5 checkpoints · crew: 1 human, 5 subagents</span></div>`;
861	      termList.classList.remove("hidden");
862	    }
863	    flashTerminal("dispatch sent — look how much got done without you", true);
864	    renderTerminal();
865	    if (winSub) {
866	      winSub.textContent = "five jobs in parallel — the report wrote itself";
867	    }
868	    winTimer = setTimeout(() => {
869	      closeTerminal(false);
870	      tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
871	      winEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
872	    }, 4000);
873	  }
874	  function submitCommand() {
875	    const n = normalizeCmd(buffer);
876	    buffer = "";
877	    if (!n) { renderTerminal(); return; }
878	    if (/^entire dispatch$/.test(n)) { sendDispatch(); return; }
879	    if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) list$/.test(n)) {
880	      // L1's command still works — the raw log, then the nudge to report.
881	      if (termList) {
882	        termList.innerHTML = sites.map((s) =>
883	          `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-id tl-id-short">${s.sys.ckpt}</span>` +
884	          `<span class="tl-title">subagent fix: ${s.sys.name.toLowerCase()} → ${s.sys.became}</span></div>`
885	        ).join("");
886	        termList.classList.remove("hidden");
887	      }
888	      flashTerminal("the raw log — now turn it into the day's report:  entire dispatch", true);
889	      renderTerminal();
890	      return;
891	    }
892	    flashTerminal("command not recognized — try:  entire dispatch", false);
893	    renderTerminal();
894	  }
895	
896	  // ---------- input ----------
897	  function onCanvasClick() {
898	    if (!started || failed) return;
899	    if (active && !fp.isLocked && !terminalOpen) fp.lock();
900	  }
901	  function onKeyDown(e) {
902	    if (!active) return;
903	
904	    // Briefing is up — Enter/Space begins the run, nothing else.
905	    if (!started) {
906	      if (e.code === "Enter" || e.code === "Space") { startLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
907	      return;
908	    }
909	
910	    // The clock ran out — only R (retry) does anything.
911	    if (failed) {
912	      if (e.code === "KeyR") { resetLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
913	      return;
914	    }
915	
916	    // While the terminal is open, all keys feed it.
917	    if (terminalOpen) {
918	      if (e.code === "Escape") { closeTerminal(true); e.preventDefault(); return; }
919	      if (termMode === "review") {
920	        if (!explained && e.code === "Enter") { runExplain(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
921	        if (explained && (e.key === "y" || e.key === "Y")) { addToShip(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
922	        e.preventDefault();
923	        return;
924	      }
925	      // report mode — type the dispatch command (the finish line)
926	      if (reportSent) { e.preventDefault(); return; }  // only Esc closes the report
927	      if (e.code === "Enter") { submitCommand(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
928	      if (e.code === "Backspace") { buffer = buffer.slice(0, -1); renderTerminal(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
929	      if (e.key && e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
930	        buffer += e.key;
931	        renderTerminal();
932	        e.preventDefault();
933	      }
934	      return;
935	    }
936	
937	    // Walking around.
938	    if (e.code === "KeyE" && nearSite?.state === "broken") { assignDrone(nearSite); e.preventDefault(); return; }
939	    if (e.code === "KeyM") { setMap(!overhead.on); e.preventDefault(); return; }
940	    if (e.code === "Escape" && fp.isLocked) fp.unlock();
941	    if (e.code === "KeyB") onExit?.();
942	  }
943	  fp.controls.addEventListener("lock", () => crosshair?.classList.remove("hidden"));
944	  fp.controls.addEventListener("unlock", () => crosshair?.classList.add("hidden"));
945	
946	  // ---------- HUD per-frame ----------
947	  let nearSite = null;
948	  let nearConsole = false;
949	
950	  function refreshHud() {
951	    if (!started || terminalOpen) {
952	      setPrompt(null);
953	      hideControls();
954	      return;
955	    }
956	    setControls(fp.isLocked);
957	    if (overhead.on) { setPrompt("Bird's-eye view — ↑↓←→ to move · M to return"); return; }
958	    if (!fp.isLocked) { setPrompt("Click to look around"); return; }
959	    if (reportSent) { setPrompt("The day is dispatched — press B to return to orbit"); return; }
960	    if (nearSite) {
961	      if (nearSite.state === "broken") setPrompt(`${nearSite.sys.name} is down — press E to send a subagent`);
962	      else if (nearSite.state === "working") setPrompt("Subagent at work — you're free, go assign another");
963	      else if (nearSite.state === "added" && !allAdded()) setPrompt(`${nearSite.sys.became} online — accepted into the ship`);
964	      return;
965	    }
966	    if (allAdded()) { setPrompt("Walk to any online system — send the day's report: `entire dispatch`"); return; }
967	    if (sites.some((s) => s.state === "sealed")) {
968	      setPrompt("An ice-blue beam means finished work — go review it");
969	      return;
970	    }
971	    if (sites.some((s) => s.state === "broken")) {
972	      setPrompt("Amber beams are broken systems — send the subagents (E)");
973	      return;
974	    }
975	    setPrompt("The fleet is working — wait for an ice-blue beam, then go review");
976	  }
977	
978	  // ---------- per-frame ----------
979	  function update(dt, t) {
980	    fp.update(dt);
981	
982	    // Level countdown — one clock for the whole run.
983	    if (active && timerRunning && !failed && !reportSent) {
984	      timeLeft = Math.max(0, timeLeft - dt);
985	      updateClock();
986	      if (timeLeft <= 0) failLevel();
987	    }
988	    applyPanicSky();
989	
990	    // Drones fly, work, and head home.
991	    for (let i = 0; i < drones.length; i++) {
992	      const d = drones[i];
993	      const beam = workBeams[i];
994	      if (d.state === "bay") {
995	        d.model.position.y = d.home.y + Math.sin((t + d.bobOff) * 2) * 0.15;
996	        d.model.userData.ring.rotation.z += dt * 1.2;
997	      } else if (d.state === "flying" || d.state === "returning") {
998	        d.t += dt;
999	        const k = Math.min(1, d.t / d.dur);
1000	        const ease = k * k * (3 - 2 * k);
1001	        d.model.position.lerpVectors(d.from, d.to, ease);
1002	        d.model.position.y += Math.sin(k * Math.PI) * 3;   // arc over the island
1003	        d.model.userData.ring.rotation.z += dt * 6;
1004	        if (k >= 1) {
1005	          if (d.state === "flying") d.state = "working";
1006	          else { d.state = "bay"; d.model.position.copy(d.home); }
1007	        }
1008	      } else if (d.state === "working") {
1009	        const s = d.site;
1010	        d.model.position.set(
1011	          s.anchor.position.x + Math.cos(t * 1.4) * 1.2,
1012	          s.anchor.position.y + 6.5 + Math.sin(t * 2.2) * 0.4,
1013	          s.anchor.position.z + Math.sin(t * 1.4) * 1.2
1014	        );
1015	        d.model.userData.ring.rotation.z += dt * 9;
1016	        beam.visible = true;
1017	        beam.position.set(d.model.position.x, d.model.position.y - 3.2, d.model.position.z);
1018	        beam.material.opacity = 0.35 + Math.sin(t * 11) * 0.2;
1019	
1020	        // The actual work happens whether you watch or not.
1021	        if (active && started && !failed) {
1022	          s.workT += dt;
1023	          if (s.workT >= WORK_TIME) {
1024	            sealSite(s);
1025	            beam.visible = false;
1026	            d.state = "returning";
1027	            d.site = null;
1028	            d.from.copy(d.model.position);
1029	            d.to.copy(d.home);
1030	            d.t = 0;
1031	            d.dur = Math.max(1, d.from.distanceTo(d.to) / DRONE_SPEED);
1032	          }
1033	        }
1034	      }
1035	    }
1036	
1037	    // Sites: status beams, warning blinks, seals, and live upgrades.
1038	    for (const s of sites) {
1039	      if (s.state === "broken") {
1040	        s.beam.material.color.setHex(0xff8a5c);
1041	        s.beam.material.opacity = 0.24 + Math.sin(t * 1.6 + s.idx) * 0.08;
1042	        s.beam.visible = true;
1043	        const warn = s.brokenModel.userData.warn;
1044	        if (warn) warn.material.emissiveIntensity = Math.sin(t * 5 + s.idx) > 0 ? 1.8 : 0.2;
1045	      } else if (s.state === "working") {
1046	        s.beam.material.color.setHex(0x6fe3ff);
1047	        s.beam.material.opacity = 0.4 + Math.sin(t * 6) * 0.18;
1048	        s.beam.visible = true;
1049	      } else if (s.state === "sealed") {
1050	        s.beam.material.color.setHex(0x8fe3ff);
1051	        s.beam.material.opacity = 0.42;
1052	        s.beam.visible = true;
1053	      } else {
1054	        s.beam.visible = false;       // added — the upgrade itself is the landmark
1055	        if (s.meltT > 0) {            // the checkpoint seal melts away
1056	          s.meltT = Math.max(0, s.meltT - dt);
1057	          s.ice.material.opacity = 0.62 * (s.meltT / MELT_DUR);
1058	          if (s.meltT <= 0) s.ice.visible = false;
1059	        }
1060	        // The improvised upgrades live a little.
1061	        const anim = s.upgradeModel.userData.anim;
1062	        if (anim?.type === "ring") {
1063	          anim.ring.rotation.y += dt * 0.8;
1064	          anim.ring.position.y = 3.4 + Math.sin(t * 1.6) * 0.2;
1065	        } else if (anim?.type === "dome") {
1066	          anim.field.rotation.y += dt * 0.25;
1067	          anim.orbit.rotation.z += dt * 0.6;
1068	        } else if (anim?.type === "skid") {
1069	          anim.pad.position.y = 1.6 + Math.sin(t * 1.8) * 0.25;
1070	        }
1071	      }
1072	    }
1073	
1074	    // Proximity: nearest site, and the ship console.
1075	    nearSite = null;
1076	    let nd = Infinity;
1077	    for (const s of sites) {
1078	      const d = Math.hypot(
1079	        camera.position.x - s.anchor.position.x,
1080	        camera.position.z - s.anchor.position.z
1081	      );
1082	      if (d < nd) { nd = d; if (d <= INTERACT_DIST) nearSite = s; }
1083	    }
1084	    nearConsole = Math.hypot(
1085	      camera.position.x - CONSOLE_POS.x, camera.position.z - CONSOLE_POS.z
1086	    ) <= CONSOLE_DIST;
1087	    consoleGlow.intensity = allAdded() && !reportSent ? 8 + Math.sin(t * 4) * 3 : 5;
1088	
1089	    // Terminals open themselves at the right places, like Level 1. After the
1090	    // fifth accept the report prompt is already open in place; if the player
1091	    // wanders off, any ONLINE system (or the bay console) reopens it.
1092	    const atReportSpot = nearConsole || nearSite?.state === "added";
1093	    if (active && started && !failed) {
1094	      if (dismissedSite && nearSite !== dismissedSite) dismissedSite = null;
1095	      if (reportDismissed && !atReportSpot) reportDismissed = false;
1096	      if (!terminalOpen) {
1097	        if (nearSite?.state === "sealed" && nearSite !== dismissedSite) {
1098	          openTerminal("review", nearSite);
1099	        } else if (atReportSpot && allAdded() && !reportSent && !reportDismissed) {
1100	          openTerminal("report", null);
1101	        }
1102	      } else if (termMode === "review" &&
1103	                 (nearSite !== reviewSite || reviewSite.state !== "sealed")) {
1104	        // walked away mid-review (ADD closes it itself)
1105	        if (nearSite !== reviewSite) closeTerminal(false);
1106	      } else if (termMode === "report" && !atReportSpot && !reportSent) {
1107	        closeTerminal(false);
1108	      }
1109	    }
1110	
1111	    overhead.update(t);
1112	    if (active) refreshHud();
1113	  }
1114	
1115	  // ---------- lifecycle ----------
1116	  function enter() {
1117	    active = true;
1118	    fp.groundAt(0, terrain.radius * 0.75);
1119	    camera.lookAt(CONSOLE_POS.x, terrain.heightAt(CONSOLE_POS.x, CONSOLE_POS.z) + 5, CONSOLE_POS.z);
1120	    canvas.addEventListener("click", onCanvasClick);
1121	    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
1122	    dbHud?.classList.remove("hidden");
1123	    fpShared?.classList.remove("hidden");
1124	    setControls(false);
1125	    crosshair?.classList.add("hidden");
1126	    updateSystems();
1127	    if (!started) {
1128	      showBriefing();
1129	    } else if (reportSent) {
1130	      fp.attach();
1131	      winEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
1132	    } else if (failed) {
1133	      resetLevel();                 // came back after a wipe → fresh run
1134	    } else {
1135	      fp.attach();
1136	      timerRunning = true;          // resume the level clock
1137	      showTutorial("The subagents kept working while you were in orbit.", 5000);
1138	    }
1139	    updateClock();
1140	    applyPanicSky();
1141	  }
1142	  function exit() {
1143	    active = false;
1144	    timerRunning = false;           // pause the clock while in orbit
1145	    setMap(false);
1146	    closeTerminal(false);
1147	    clearTimeout(winTimer);
1148	    fp.unlock();
1149	    fp.detach();
1150	    canvas.removeEventListener("click", onCanvasClick);
1151	    window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
1152	    setPrompt(null);
1153	    hideControls();
1154	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
1155	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
1156	    failEl?.classList.add("hidden");
1157	    winEl?.classList.add("hidden");
1158	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
1159	    dbHud?.classList.add("hidden");
1160	    fpShared?.classList.add("hidden");
1161	    crosshair?.classList.add("hidden");
1162	  }
1163	  function resize() {
1164	    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
1165	    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
1166	    overhead.resize();
1167	  }
1168	
1169	  return {
1170	    scene,
1171	    get camera() { return overhead.on ? overhead.camera : camera; },
1172	    update, enter, exit, resize,
1173	  };
1174	}
1175
```

```
// Catalog of fragments scattered on the planet. Each seeds a future

export const FRAGMENTS = [
  {
    kind: "helmet",
    lat: 28, lon: -52,
    title: "Abandoned EVA Helmet",
    body: "The visor is fogged from the inside. Its tiny logger still loops one phrase: a recap that started but never finished. Whoever wore this was trying to remember something — fast.",
    hint: "entire recap",
  },
  {
    kind: "logbook",
    lat: -14, lon: 122,
    title: "Torn Logbook Page",
    body: "Coordinates, half-erased by rain. Beneath the smudged ink a single checkpoint marker bleeds through — as if someone wanted a way back to this exact moment.",
    hint: "entire checkpoint",
  },
  {
    kind: "panel",
    lat: 52, lon: 18,
    title: "Cracked Nav Panel",
    body: "Diagnostics flatlined mid-write. The activity trail scrolls up the shattered glass and then simply… stops. The last legible line is a timestamp from the night of the crash.",
    hint: "entire activity",
  },
  {
    kind: "beacon",
    lat: -40, lon: -120,
    title: "Signal Beacon",
    body: "Still pulsing, somehow. It's been broadcasting the same dispatch into the dark for days — a summary of work no one is left to read.",
    hint: "entire dispatch",
  },
];

// Convert lat/lon (degrees) to a point on a sphere of the given radius.
function latLonToVec3(lat, lon, radius) {
  const phi = (90 - lat) * (Math.PI / 180);
  const theta = (lon + 180) * (Math.PI / 180);
  return new THREE.Vector3(
    -radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta),
    radius * Math.cos(phi),
    radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta)
  );
}
```

```
@keyframes meter-glow {
  0%, 100% { filter: brightness(1); }
  50% { filter: brightness(1.4); }
}

/* Level countdown clock (top-left, under the PLANETFALL title).
   Shared by Level 1 (#countdown), the Drone Bay (#db-countdown), and the
   shelved Archive level (#l2-countdown). */
#countdown,
#l2-countdown,
#db-countdown {
  position: absolute;
  top: 78px;
  left: 32px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 11px;
  padding: 8px 16px;
  background: rgba(6, 11, 22, 0.78);
  border: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.4);
  border-radius: 9px;
  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
  transition: border-color 0.3s ease, background 0.3s ease;
}

.cd-label {
  font-size: 10.5px;
  letter-spacing: 0.24em;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--accent);
  opacity: 0.95;
}

#countdown-time,
#l2-countdown-time,
#db-countdown-time {
  font-family: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, monospace;
  font-size: 27px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  color: var(--ink);
  min-width: 66px;
  text-align: right;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

/* Urgent state — the clock is running low */
#countdown.is-low,
#l2-countdown.is-low,
#db-countdown.is-low {
  border-color: rgba(255, 90, 90, 0.7);
  background: rgba(40, 10, 12, 0.6);
  animation: cd-pulse 1s ease-in-out infinite;
}
#countdown.is-low .cd-label,
#l2-countdown.is-low .cd-label,
#db-countdown.is-low .cd-label { color: #ff7a6b; }
#countdown.is-low #countdown-time,
#l2-countdown.is-low #l2-countdown-time,
#db-countdown.is-low #db-countdown-time { color: #ff8a78; }

@keyframes cd-pulse {
  0%, 100% { box-shadow: 0 0 0 rgba(255, 90, 90, 0); }
  50% { box-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(255, 90, 90, 0.55); }
}

/* Critical state — under a few seconds, the clock goes frantic */
#countdown.is-critical,
#l2-countdown.is-critical,
#db-countdown.is-critical {
  border-color: rgba(255, 60, 60, 0.95);
  background: rgba(60, 8, 10, 0.72);
  animation: cd-panic 0.5s steps(2, end) infinite;
}
#countdown.is-critical .cd-label,
#l2-countdown.is-critical .cd-label,
#db-countdown.is-critical .cd-label { color: #ff4d4d; }
#countdown.is-critical #countdown-time,
#l2-countdown.is-critical #l2-countdown-time,
#db-countdown.is-critical #db-countdown-time {
  color: #ff5a4d;
  text-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(255, 70, 60, 0.9);
}

@keyframes cd-panic {
  0% { box-shadow: 0 0 22px rgba(255, 50, 50, 0.8); transform: translateX(-1px); }
  100% { box-shadow: 0 0 8px rgba(255, 50, 50, 0.4); transform: translateX(1px); }
}

/* Time-out fail screen */
/* ---------- landing briefing modal ---------- */
#briefing,
#l2-briefing,
#db-briefing {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 30;
  pointer-events: auto;     /* #ui is pointer-events:none — re-enable for the modal */
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 24px;
  background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 30%, rgba(20, 16, 44, 0.62), rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.86));
  backdrop-filter: blur(7px);
  animation: bf-fade 0.5s ease both;
}
#briefing.hidden,
#l2-briefing.hidden,
#db-briefing.hidden { display: none; }

@keyframes bf-fade {
  from { opacity: 0; }
  to { opacity: 1; }
}

.bf-card {
  width: min(540px, 92vw);
  max-height: 88vh;
  overflow-y: auto;
  padding: 30px 34px 26px;
  text-align: left;
  background: var(--panel-bg);
  border: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.28);
  border-radius: 16px;
  box-shadow: 0 26px 80px rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.7), 0 0 50px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.12);
  animation: bf-rise 0.55s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) both;
```

```
/* ---------- Level 2 HUD ("The Archive") ---------- */

/* The ship's current request — the keyword hides in plain sight */
#l2-request {
  position: absolute;
  top: 136px;
  left: 32px;
  width: min(310px, calc(100vw - 64px));
  padding: 13px 16px;
  background: rgba(6, 11, 22, 0.78);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.4);
  border-radius: 9px;
  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
}
.rq-head {
  font-size: 10px;
  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--accent-warm);
  margin-bottom: 8px;
}
#l2-req-count { font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.rq-text {
  font-size: 12.5px;
  line-height: 1.55;
  color: var(--ink);
  opacity: 0.92;
}

/* ---------- Level 2 HUD ("The Drone Bay") ---------- */

/* Fleet status panel — five systems, four states */
#db-systems {
  position: absolute;
  top: 136px;
  left: 32px;
  width: min(290px, calc(100vw - 64px));
  padding: 12px 14px;
  background: rgba(6, 11, 22, 0.78);

}
.db-sys {
  display: flex;
  align-items: baseline;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 3px 0;
  font-size: 11px;
  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
}
.db-sys-name { color: var(--ink); opacity: 0.85; white-space: nowrap; }
.db-sys-state {
  font-size: 9.5px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  white-space: nowrap;
}
.db-sys.is-broken .db-sys-state { color: #ff8a5c; }
.db-sys.is-working .db-sys-state { color: var(--accent); animation: db-work-blink 1.1s ease-in-out infinite; }
.db-sys.is-sealed .db-sys-state { color: #bfe9ff; }
.db-sys.is-added .db-sys-state { color: #7cffb0; }
.db-sys.is-broken .db-sys-name { opacity: 0.6; }
@keyframes db-work-blink { 50% { opacity: 0.45; } }

/* All three memories made it home */
#l2-win,
#db-win {
```

```
  margin-top: 10px;

}

/* Ship terminal — type the real commands. pointer-events back on so the
   Drone Bay's ADD TO SHIP button is clickable (#ui is pointer-events:none). */
#terminal {
  position: absolute;
  pointer-events: auto;
  bottom: 70px;
  left: 50%;
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  width: min(560px, calc(100vw - 40px));
  padding: 14px 16px;
  background: rgba(4, 10, 18, 0.88);
  border: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.42);
  border-radius: 8px;
  box-shadow: 0 14px 40px rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.55), 0 0 24px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.12);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  text-align: left;
}

#term-hint {
  font-size: 12.5px;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  color: var(--ink);
  opacity: 0.68;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}

#term-line {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  font-size: 16px;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
}

.term-prompt {
  color: var(--accent);
  opacity: 0.9;
  margin-right: 8px;
}

#term-input { color: var(--ink); white-space: pre; }
#term-input.is-dim { color: var(--accent-warm); opacity: 0.92; }

/* Call-to-action: the actionable command/keys — the brightest thing here */
#term-cta {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  margin-top: 13px;
}
#term-cta:empty { display: none; }
.cta-label {
  font-size: 10px;
  letter-spacing: 0.24em;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--accent);
  opacity: 0.9;
}
.cta-cmd {
  font-family: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, monospace;
  font-size: 16px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  color: #04121a;
  padding: 5px 14px;
  border-radius: 7px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #6fe3ff, #9af0ff);
  box-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.45);
}
.cta-key {
  display: inline-grid;
  place-items: center;
  min-width: 26px;
  height: 26px;
  padding: 0 7px;
  font-family: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, monospace;
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 700;
  border-radius: 6px;
  color: #04121a;
}
.cta-key-yes {
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #6fe3ff, #9af0ff);
  box-shadow: 0 0 14px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.45);
}
.cta-key-no {
  background: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.16);
  color: var(--ink);
  border: 1px solid rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.3);
}
.cta-note {
  font-size: 12.5px;
  color: var(--ink);
  opacity: 0.82;
  margin-right: 8px;
}

/* ADD TO SHIP — the accept button after an explain (Drone Bay) */
#db-add-btn {
  padding: 8px 18px;
  font-family: inherit;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  color: #04121a;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #5cffb0, #bfffe0);
  border: none;
  border-radius: 7px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(92, 255, 176, 0.45);
  transition: transform 0.12s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;
}
#db-add-btn:hover {
  transform: translateY(-1px);
  box-shadow: 0 0 26px rgba(92, 255, 176, 0.65);
}
#db-add-btn:active { transform: translateY(0); }

/* `entire checkpoint list` / search output. Level 2's full archive listing is
   long on purpose — it scrolls, and that's the lesson. */
#term-list:not(.hidden) {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 5px;
  margin-top: 11px;
  padding-top: 10px;
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.16);
  max-height: 168px;
  overflow-y: auto;
}
.term-list-row {
  display: flex;
  gap: 12px;
  font-size: 12.5px;
  line-height: 1.3;
}
.tl-id { color: var(--accent); min-width: 300px; white-space: nowrap; }
.tl-key { color: var(--ink); opacity: 0.55; }
.tl-title { color: var(--ink); opacity: 0.82; }

/* Level 2 archive rows: just id + summary, so the id column stays compact. */
.tl-id.tl-id-short { min-width: 110px; }
/* Explain output rows: key/value pairs (Drone Bay review cards). */
.tl-exp-key { min-width: 90px; flex: none; }

.term-cursor {
  display: inline-block;
  width: 8px;
  height: 16px;
  background: var(--accent);
  animation: term-blink 1s steps(1) infinite;
}

@keyframes term-blink { 50% { opacity: 0; } }
```

```json
  "content": "import * as THREE from \"three\";\nimport { createTerrain } from \"./terrain.js\";\nimport { makeBeamTexture } from \"./memoryProps.js\";\nimport { SYSTEMS, UPGRADE_BUILDERS } from \"./droneBayView.js\";\n\n// LEVEL 3 (\"Launch Clearance\") — the finale. The player sits in the pilot's chair for the first time, and the\n// whole level happens at one console — no walking.\n//\n// The launch computer won't arm on work nobody can account for, and most of\n// today's work the player never watched (the subagents did it). Failure lives in the launch window, nowhere else.\n\nconst TOTAL_TIME = 150;      // seconds of launch window (tunable)\nconst LOW_TIME = 45;         // clock turns urgent under this\nconst CRIT_TIME = 15; // 8\n\n// ---------- the five pre-flight questions ----------\n// Each tool: label (what the menu shows), echo (what \"runs\" at the prompt),\n// rows ([key, value] output lines), ok (false = dead end), note (the flash).\n// `site` = which upgrade flares on confirm (index into SYSTEMS, or null).\nfunction cardRows(sys) {\n  return [[\"checkpoint\", sys.ckpt], ...sys.card];\n}\nconst ANTENNA = SYSTEMS[2], COILS = SYSTEMS[0], STRUTS = SYSTEMS[4];\n\nconst QUESTIONS = [\n  {\n    q: \"First check — the long-range antenna. The log says the rebuild wasn't simple. How many attempts did it take?\",\n    site: 2,\n    tools: [\n      {\n        label: `entire checkpoint explain ${ANTENNA.ckpt}`,\n        echo: `entire checkpoint explain ${ANTENNA.ckpt}`,\n        ok: true, rows: cardRows(ANTENNA),\n        note: \"the raw record — the answer is in there\",\n      },\n      {\n        label: \"Skill: what-happened — ask in plain language\",\n        echo: \"what happened to the antenna?\",\n        ok: true, rows: [\n          [\"ran\", `entire checkpoint explain ${ANTENNA.ckpt}`],\n          [\"answer\", \"subagent-3 respun the mast into a signal spire — it took 3 attempts; the first two fell over.\"],\n        ],\n        note: \"the skill ran the command for you\",\n      },\n      {\n        label: \"entire dispatch\",\n        echo: \"entire dispatch\",\n        ok: false, rows: [\n          [\"DISPATCH\", \"day report — 5 systems repaired, 5 checkpoints filed\"],\n        ],\n        note: \"the day's report counts systems, not attempts — try another way\",\n      },\n    ],\n    answers: [\"2\", \"3\", \"5\"], correct: 1,\n  },\n  {\n    q: \"Something on this ship is built from salvaged hull plate. tools: [\n      {\n        label: \"entire checkpoint list\",\n        echo: \"entire checkpoint list\",\n        ok: true, rows: [\n          ...L1_RECORD,\n          ...SYSTEMS.map((s) => [s.ckpt, The frame is simple slabs — enough to say\n  // \"you're inside the ship\" without modeling a ship.\n  const rig = new THREE.Group();\n  const rigGroundY = terrain.heightAt(RIG_POS.x, RIG_POS.z);\n  rig.position.set(RIG_POS.x, rigGroundY + RIG_EYE, RIG_POS.z);\n  rig.add(camera);\n  scene.add(rig);\n  camera.lookAt(0, terrain.heightAt(0, 0) + 4, -8);\n  const baseRotX = camera.rotation.x;\n  const baseRotY = camera.rotation.y;\n\n  const HULL = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x141826, metalness: 0.55, roughness: 0.5 });\n  const cockpit = new THREE.Group();\n  const dash = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.6, 0.5, 0.8), HULL);\n  dash.position.set(0, -1.06, -1.55);\n  dash.rotation.x = 0.22;\n  const dashGlow = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.0, 0.05, 0.05),\n    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.85 })\n  );\n  dashGlow.position.set(0, -0.82, -1.42);\n  const header = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.6, 0.42, 0.12), HULL);\n  header.position.set(0, 1.28, -1.7);\n  const pillarL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.2, 2.6, 0.12), HULL);\n  pillarL.position.set(-1.72, 0.1, -1.7);\n  pillarL.rotation.z = 0.1;\n  const pillarR = pillarL.clone();\n  pillarR.position.x = 1.72;\n  pillarR.rotation.z = -0.1;\n  cockpit.add(dash, dashGlow, header, pillarL, pillarR);\n  camera.add(cockpit);\n  const cabinLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x6fe3ff, 1.4, 7, 2);\n  cabinLight.position.set(0, 0.2, -0.6);\n  camera.add(cabinLight);\n\n  // Stars for the climb out — invisible until the sky turns to space.\n  const starGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n  const starPts = new Float32Array(700 * 3);\n  for (let i = 0; i < 700; i++) {\n    const v = new THREE.Vector3().randomDirection().multiplyScalar(950);\n    starPts[i * 3] = v.x; starPts[i * 3 + 1] = Math.abs(v.y); starPts[i * 3 + 2] = v.z;\n  }\n  starGeo.setAttribute(\"position\", new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPts, 3));\n  const starMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n    color: 0xdfe9ff, size: 2.2, transparent: true, opacity: 0,\n    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, fog: false,\n    sizeAttenuation: true,\n  });\n  const stars = new THREE.Points(starGeo, starMat);\n  scene.add(stars);\n\n  // ---------- HUD elements ----------\n  const hud = document.getElementById(\"lc-hud\");\n  const briefingEl = document.getElementById(\"lc-briefing\");\n  const briefingStartBtn = document.getElementById(\"lc-briefing-start\");\n  const countdownEl = document.getElementById(\"lc-countdown\");\n  const countdownTime = document.getElementById(\"lc-countdown-time\");\n  const codeSegsEl = document.getElementById(\"lc-code-segs\");\n  const consoleEl = document.getElementById(\"lc-console\");\n  const speakerEl = document.getElementById(\"lc-speaker\");\n  const questionEl = document.getElementById(\"lc-question\");\n  const menuEl = document.getElementById(\"lc-menu\");\n  const outputEl = document.getElementById(\"lc-output\");\n  const answersEl = document.getElementById(\"lc-answers\");\n  const msgEl = document.getElementById(\"lc-msg\");\n  const winEl = document.getElementById(\"lc-win\");\n  const winSub = document.getElementById(\"lc-win-sub\");\n  const failEl = document.getElementById(\"lc-fail\");\n\n  let active = false;\n  let started = false;\n  let failed = false;\n  let launched = false; SYSTEMS[Q.site].ckpt.slice(0, 4) : \"8/8 \";\n  });\n  function renderCode() {\n    if (!codeSegsEl) return;\n    const labels = segLabels();\n    codeSegsEl.innerHTML = QUESTIONS.map((_, i) =>\n      `<span class=\"lc-seg${labels[i] ? \" is-locked\" : \"\"}\">${labels[i] ?? \"····\"}</span>`\n    ).join(\"\");\n  }\n\n  // ---------- console rendering ----------\n  function flashMsg(text, ok) {\n    if (!msgEl) return;\n    clearTimeout(msgTimer);\n    msgEl.textContent = text;\n    msgEl.classList.remove(\"show-ok\", \"show-err\");\n    msgEl.classList.add(ok ? \"show-ok\" : \"show-err\");\n    msgTimer = setTimeout(() => msgEl.classList.remove(\"show-ok\", \"show-err\"), 3600);\n  }\n  function renderOutput(echo, rows, isSkill) {\n    if (!outputEl) return;\n    outputEl.innerHTML =\n      `<div class=\"lc-row lc-echo\"><span class=\"lc-prompt\">${isSkill ? \"you:\" : \"crashlog:~$\"}</span>` +\n      `<span class=\"lc-cmd\">${echo}</span></div>` +\n      rows.map(([k, v]) =>\n        `<div class=\"lc-row\"><span class=\"lc-k\">${k}</span><span class=\"lc-v\">${v}</span></div>`\n      ).join(\"\");\n    outputEl.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function renderQuestion() {\n    const Q = QUESTIONS[qIndex];\n    if (speakerEl) speakerEl.textContent = `SHIP · PRE-FLIGHT ${qIndex + 1} / ${QUESTIONS.length}`;\n    if (questionEl) questionEl.textContent = Q.q;\n    outputEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    if (outputEl) outputEl.innerHTML = \"\";\n    answersEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    if (answersEl) answersEl.innerHTML = \"\";\n    if (Q.single) {\n      phase = \"single\";\n      if (menuEl) {\n        menuEl.innerHTML =\n          `<div class=\"lc-single\"><span class=\"lc-prompt\">crashlog:~$</span>` +\n          `<span cta-key-yes\">Enter</kbd><span class=\"cta-note\">file it</span></div>`;\n        menuEl.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n      }\n    } else {\n      phase = \"menu\";\n      renderMenu();\n    }\n  }\n  function renderMenu() {\n    const Q = QUESTIONS[qIndex];\n    if (!menuEl) return;\n    menuEl.innerHTML = Q.tools.map((tool, i) => {\n      const dead = deadTools.has(i);\n      const used = usedTool === i;\n      const skill = tool.label.startsWith(\"Skill:\");\n      return `<button type=\"button\" class=\"lc-opt${dead ? \" is-dead\" : \"\"}${used ? \" is-used\" : \"\"}` +\n menuEl.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function renderAnswers() {\n    const Q = QUESTIONS[qIndex];\n    if (!answersEl) return;\n    answersEl.innerHTML =\n      `<div function pickTool(i) {\n    if (phase !== \"menu\") return;\n    const Q = QUESTIONS[qIndex];\n    const tool = Q.tools[i];\n    if (!tool || deadTools.has(i)) return;\n    const isSkill = wrongChips.add(i);\n      flashMsg(\"not what the record says — read it again\", false);\n      renderAnswers();\n      return;\n    }\n    confirmQuestion();\n  }\n  function confirmQuestion() {\n    const Q = QUESTIONS[qIndex];\n    Q.done = true;\n    phase = \"done\";\n    if (Q.site != null) {\n      const s = sites[Q.site];\n      s.confirmed = true;\n      s.flareT = 0;\n      s.beam.visible  answersEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    menuEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    if (qIndex + 1 < QUESTIONS.length) {\n      nextTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n        qIndex += 1;\n        usedTool = || launched) return;\n    const Q = QUESTIONS[qIndex];\n    Q.done = true;\n    phase = \"done\";\n    renderOutput(Q.single.label, Q.single.rows, false);\n    menuEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    renderCode();\n    flashMsg(\"flight log filed — ignition\", true);\n    timerRunning = false;\n    countdownEl?.classList.remove(\"is-low\", \"is-critical\");\n    for (const s of sites) { Math.sin(performance.now() / 85);\n      p += 0.14 * throb;\n    }\n    return Math.min(1, p);\n  }\n  function applySky() {\n    if (launched || won) {\n      const k = Math.min(1, rig.position.y / starMat.opacity = k;\n      return;\n    }\n    const p = panicFactor();\n    scene.background.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(SKY_PANIC, p);\n    scene.fog.color.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(FOG_PANIC, p);\n    dome.material.color.copy(DOME_CALM).lerp(DOME_PANIC, p);\n    sun.color.copy(SUN_CALM).lerp(SUN_PANIC, p * 0.85);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- briefing / win / fail / reset ----------\n  function started = true;\n    briefingEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    consoleEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;\n    timerRunning = true;\n    updateClock();\n    renderCode();\n function resetLevel() {\n    failed = false;\n    launched = false;\n    won = false;\n    liftT = 0;\n    qIndex = 0;\n    phase = \"menu\";\n    usedTool = null;\n    deadTools = new Set();\n
```
