starMat.opacity = 0;\n    sun.intensity = 1.5;\n    scene.fog.far = 420;\n    failEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    winEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    consoleEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;\n    timerRunning = true;\n    updateClock();\n    renderCode();\n    renderQuestion();\n  }\n\n  // ---------- input ----------\n  function onKeyDown(e) {\n    if (!active) anim.ring.position.y = 3.4 + Math.sin(t * 1.6) * 0.2;\n      } else if (anim?.type === \"dome\") {\n        anim.field.rotation.y += dt * 0.25;\n        anim.orbit.rotation.z += dt * 0.6;\n      } else if (anim?.type === \"skid\") {\n        anim.pad.position.y = 1.6 + Math.sin(t * 1.8) * 0.25;\n      }\n      // Gold confirm flare: quick bloom, then settles to a steady glow.\n      if (s.confirmed) {\n        s.flareT += dt;\n        const settle = 0.28 + Math.sin(t * 2.2 + s.idx) * 0.04;\n        const bloom = Math.max(0, 1 - s.flareT / 2.5) * 0.5;\n        s.beam.material.opacity = settle + bloom;\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (launched && !won) {\n      // Ignition: the island falls away, the sky becomes space.\n      liftT += dt;\n      const v = Math.min(46, 6 + liftT * 9);\n * 320);\n      sun.intensity = Math.max(0.6, 1.5 - liftT * 0.1);\n      if (liftT >= LIFT_DUR) {\n        won = true;\n        if (winSub) winSub.textContent =\n          `${RECORD_TOTAL} checkpoints · ${QUESTIONS.length} questions · 0 guesses`;\n        winEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n      }\n    } else if (!failed && !won) {\n      // Idle cockpit sway — alive, not nauseating.\n      camera.rotation.x = baseRotX + Math.sin(t * 0.7) * 0.006;\n      camera.rotation.y = baseRotY + Math.sin(t * 0.45) * 0.008;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // ---------- lifecycle ----------\n  function enter() {\n    active = true;\n    window.addEventListener(\"keydown\", onKeyDown);\n    hud?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    if (!started) {\n      showBriefing();\n    } else if (won) {\n      winEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    } else if (failed) {\n      resetLevel();                 // came back after a miss → fresh window\n    } else if (!launched) {\n }\n  function resize() {\n    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;\n    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n  }\n\n  return { scene, camera, update, enter, exit, resize };\n}\n"

{
  "command": "cat >> src/style.css <<'EOF'\n\n/* ---------- Level 3 HUD (\"Launch Clearance\") ---------- */\n\n/* The briefing quotes the ship's AI — set the line apart. */\n.bf-quote {\n  font-family: ui-monospace, \"SF Mono\", Menlo, monospace;\n  font-size: 12.5px;\n  color: var(--accent);\n  border-left: 2px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.45);\n  padding-left: 12px;\n  margin-top: 10px;\n}\n\n/* The launch code — one segment per confirmed answer */\n#lc-code {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 136px;\n  left: 32px;\n  padding: 12px 14px;\n  background: rgba(6, 11, 22, 0.78);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.4);\n  border-radius: 9px;\n  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);\n}\n.lc-code-head {\n  font-size: 10px;\n  letter-spacing: 0.22em;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: var(--accent-warm);\n  margin-bottom: 9px;\n}\n#lc-code-segs {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 6px;\n}\n.lc-seg {\n  display: inline-grid;\n  place-items: center;\n  min-width: 44px;\n  height: 26px;\n  font-family: ui-monospace, \"SF Mono\", Menlo, monospace;\n  font-size: 12px;\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\n  color: var(--ink);\n  opacity: 0.4;\n  border: 1px dashed rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.35);\n  border-radius: 5px;\n}\n.lc-seg.is-locked {\n  opacity: 1;\n  color: #1a1206;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  border: none;\n  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #ffd27a, #ffe7b0);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 14px rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.5);\n}\n\n/* The pre-flight console — the whole level happens here */\n#lc-console {\n  position: absolute;\n  pointer-events: auto;\n  bottom: 70px;\n  left: 50%;\n  transform: translateX(-50%);\n  width: min(620px, calc(100vw - 40px));\n  padding: 15px 18px;\n  background: rgba(4, 10, 18, 0.88);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.42);\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  box-shadow: 0 14px 40px rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.55), 0 0 24px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.12);\n  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);\n  text-align: left;\n}\n#lc-console.hidden { display: none; }\n\n#lc-speaker {\n  font-size: 10px;\n  letter-spacing: 0.22em;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: var(--accent);\n  margin-bottom: 7px;\n}\n#lc-question {\n  font-size: 14.5px;\n  line-height: 1.5;\n  color: var(--ink);\n  margin-bottom: 12px;\n}\n\n/* The tool menu — pick a way to look it up */\n#lc-menu:not(.hidden) {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 6px;\n}\n.lc-opt {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 10px;\n  padding: 7px 10px;\n  font-family: ui-monospace, \"SF Mono\", Menlo, monospace;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  text-align: left;\n  color: var(--ink);\n  cursor: pointer;\n  background: rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.07);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.28);\n  border-radius: 7px;\n  transition: background 0.15s ease, border-color 0.15s ease, transform 0.1s ease;\n}\n.lc-opt:hover:not(:disabled):not(.is-dead) {\n  background: rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.16);\n  border-color: rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.55);\n  transform: translateY(-1px);\n}\n.lc-opt kbd {\n  display: inline-grid;\n  place-items: center;\n  min-width: 22px;\n  height: 22px;\n  font-family: inherit;\n  font-size: 12px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: #04121a;\n  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #6fe3ff, #9af0ff);\n  border-radius: 5px;\n  flex: none;\n}\n.lc-opt.is-skill { border-color: rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.4); }\n.lc-opt.is-skill kbd { background: linear-gradient(90deg, #ffd27a, #ffe7b0); }\n.lc-opt.is-dead {\n  opacity: 0.38;\n  cursor: default;\n  text-decoration: line-through;\n}\n.lc-opt.is-used {\n  border-color: rgba(92, 255, 176, 0.5);\n  background: rgba(92, 255, 176, 0.08);\n}\n.lc-opt:disabled { cursor: default; }\n\n/* What the tool printed */\n#lc-output:not(.hidden) {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 5px;\n  margin-top: 12px;\n  padding-top: 10px;\n  border-top: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.16);\n  max-height: 178px;\n  overflow-y: auto;\n}\n.lc-row {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 12px;\n  font-size: 12.5px;\n  line-height: 1.35;\n  font-family: ui-monospace, \"SF Mono\", Menlo, monospace;\n}\n.lc-row .lc-k { color: var(--ink); opacity: 0.55; min-width: 96px; flex: none; }\n.lc-row .lc-v { color: var(--ink); opacity: 0.85; }\n.lc-echo { margin-bottom: 3px; }\n.lc-prompt { color: var(--accent); opacity: 0.9; flex: none; }\n.lc-cmd { color: var(--ink); }\n\n/* Confirm chips — the answer is on screen; click it */\n#lc-answers:not(.hidden) {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 8px;\n  margin-top: 12px;\n  padding-top: 10px;\n  border-top: 1px solid rgba(92, 255, 176, 0.2);\n}\n.lc-answers-head {\n  width: 100%;\n  font-size: 10px;\n  letter-spacing: 0.22em;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: #7cffb0;\n}\n.lc-chip {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 8px;\n  padding: 7px 12px;\n  font-family: inherit;\n  font-size: 12.5px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  letter-spacing: 0.04em;\n  color: var(--ink);\n  cursor: pointer;\n  background: rgba(92, 255, 176, 0.08);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(92, 255, 176, 0.4);\n  border-radius: 7px;\n  transition: background 0.15s ease, border-color 0.15s ease, transform 0.1s ease;\n}\n.lc-chip:hover:not(.is-wrong) {\n  background: rgba(92, 255, 176, 0.18);\n  border-color: rgba(92, 255, 176, 0.7);\n  transform: translateY(-1px);\n}\n.lc-chip kbd {\n  display: inline-grid;\n  place-items: center;\n  min-width: 20px;\n  height: 20px;\n  font-family: ui-monospace, monospace;\n  font-size: 11px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: #04121a;\n  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #5cffb0, #bfffe0);\n  border-radius: 4px;\n  flex: none;\n}\n.lc-chip.is-wrong {\n  opacity: 0.35;\n  cursor: default;\n  text-decoration: line-through;\n}\n\n/* The finale: one pre-filled command, one Enter */\n.lc-single {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 8px;\n  font-family: ui-monospace, \"SF Mono\", Menlo, monospace;\n  font-size: 16px;\n  cursor: pointer;\n}\n.lc-single .lc-cmd { color: var(--ink); }\n.lc-single-cta {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 10px;\n  margin-top: 12px;\n  cursor: pointer;\n}\n.lc-cursor {\n  display: inline-block;\n  width: 8px;\n  height: 16px;\n  background: var(--accent);\n  animation: term-blink 1s steps(1) infinite;\n}\n\n/* Confirm / dead-end flashes, same voice as the terminal's */\n#lc-msg {\n  margin-top: 10px;\n  min-height: 16px;\n  font-size: 12.5px;\n  letter-spacing: 0.02em;\n  opacity: 0;\n  transition: opacity 0.25s ease;\n}\n#lc-msg.show-ok { opacity: 0.92; color: #7cffb0; }\n#lc-msg.show-err { opacity: 0.92; color: #ff8a73; }\n\n@media (max-width: 680px) {\n  #lc-code { top: 124px; left: 16px; padding: 9px 11px; }\n  .lc-seg { min-width: 36px; height: 22px; font-size: 10.5px; }\n  #lc-console { bottom: 48px; padding: 12px 13px; }\n  #lc-question { font-size: 13px; }\n  .lc-opt { font-size: 11.5px; }\n}\nEOF\necho done",
  "description": "Append Level 3 console styles to style.css"
}

  "old_string": "const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);\nconst requestedView = params.get(\"view\");\nconst jumpToLevel2 = requestedView === \"level2\" || params.get(\"level\") === \"2\";\n\n// ---------- views ----------\n// After Level 1's `entire checkpoint list`, the ship wakes its drone bay:\n// the orbit pin glitches and landing again enters Level 2 (\"The Drone Bay\").\n// The shelved search level (\"The Archive\") stays reachable at ?view=archive.\nlet level1Done = jumpToLevel2;\n\nconst planetView = createPlanetView(renderer, {\n  onIslandClick: () => switchTo(level1Done ? droneBayView : islandView),\n});\nconst islandView = createIslandView(renderer, {\n  onExit: () => switchTo(planetView),\n  onComplete: () => { level1Done = true; },\n});\nconst droneBayView = createDroneBayView(renderer, {\n  onExit: () => switchTo(planetView),\n});\nconst archiveView = createArchiveView(renderer, {\n  onExit: () => switchTo(planetView),\n});\n\n// Default to the orbit view; ?view=island jumps straight to Level 1,\n// ?view=level2 (or ?level=2) to Level 2, ?view=archive to the shelved\n// search level — handy for development.\nlet current = requestedView === \"island\" ? islandView\n  : requestedView === \"archive\" ? archiveView\n  : jumpToLevel2 ? droneBayView : planetView;\ncurrent.enter();",
  "new_string": "const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);\nconst requestedView = params.get(\"view\");\nconst jumpToLevel2 = requestedView === \"level2\" || params.get(\"level\") === \"2\";\nconst jumpToLevel3 = requestedView === \"level3\" || params.get(\"level\") === \"3\";\n\n// ---------- views ----------\n// After Level 1's `entire checkpoint list`, the ship wakes its drone bay:\n// the orbit pin glitches and landing again enters Level 2 (\"The Drone Bay\").\n// After Level 2's `entire dispatch`, the launch window opens: landing again\n// enters Level 3 (\"Launch Clearance\") — the finale.\n// The shelved search level (\"The Archive\") stays reachable at ?view=archive.\nlet level1Done = jumpToLevel2 || jumpToLevel3;\nlet level2Done = jumpToLevel3;\n\nconst planetView = createPlanetView(renderer, {\n  onIslandClick: () => switchTo(\n    level2Done ? launchView : level1Done ? droneBayView : islandView\n  ),\n});\nconst islandView = createIslandView(renderer, {\n  onExit: () => switchTo(planetView),\n  onComplete: () => { level1Done = true; },\n});\nconst droneBayView = createDroneBayView(renderer, {\n  onExit: () => switchTo(planetView),\n  onComplete: () => { level2Done = true; },\n});\nconst archiveView = createArchiveView(renderer, {\n  onExit: () => switchTo(planetView),\n});\nconst launchView = createLaunchView(renderer, {\n  onExit: () => switchTo(planetView),\n});\n\n// Default to the orbit view; ?view=island jumps straight to Level 1,\n// ?view=level2 (or ?level=2) to Level 2, ?view=level3 (or ?level=3) to the\n// finale, ?view=archive to the shelved search level — handy for development.\nlet current = requestedView === \"island\" ? islandView\n  : requestedView === \"archive\" ? archiveView\n  : jumpToLevel3 ? launchView\n  : jumpToLevel2 ? droneBayView : planetView;\ncurrent.enter();"
}

> AGENT

Built. Level 3 "Launch Clearance" is playable at **`http://localhost:5173/?view=level3`** (or `?level=3`) — and in normal progression, finishing Level 2's `entire dispatch` now opens the launch window: the orbit hint changes and the pin boards the ship. Production build compiles clean; per our standing rule I haven't browser-tested — that's yours.

What you'll see, beat by beat:

1. **Briefing** — "LAUNCH WINDOW OPEN · LEVEL 3," the story, and your line set off as a quote: *"I can read the record. Protocol says you have to be the one to confirm it."* TAKE THE PILOT'S SEAT starts the 2:30 window.
2. **The cockpit** — first time inside the ship: window frame and glowing dash around the screen edges, gentle idle sway, your five Level 2 upgrades alive out on the island (eye height was raised until all five clear the dunes — verified in Node).
3. **Five questions** at one console, two clicks each. The menus rotate exactly as agreed — every question has two valid tools (a raw command and a skill that visibly runs the command then answers in one plain sentence) plus one struck-through-able dead end that only costs clock. The lineup: antenna attempts (explain / what-happened / dispatch-dead-end), hull plate (search / recall / explain-dead-end), record count — eight, three L1 memories plus five repairs (list / recall / search-dead-end), **why does the ship float** — the discrepancy question made the cut, "torque spec: vibes" and all — and finally `entire dispatch` files the flight log on a single pre-filled Enter.
4. **Each confirm** locks a gold launch-code segment (stamped with the answering checkpoint's first hex) and fires a gold flare beam at that system out the window. Wrong chips dim and strike through; the only real enemy is the clock, with the same panic-sky as L1/L2.
5. **The fifth confirm → ignition** — nine seconds of the island falling away, sky lerping to space, stars coming in — then **LIFTOFF**, with "every question had an answer in the record. You weren't there — your checkpoints were." and the `npx skills add https://github.com/entireio/skills` line.

Fail is "LAUNCH WINDOW MISSED," R for a fresh window. Tuning knobs are all top-of-file in `launchView.js`: `TOTAL_TIME` (150s), `NEXT_DELAY` between questions, `LIFT_DUR` for the climb-out.

PLAN.md is updated to "agreed + built, pending playtest" with the build decisions recorded. Nothing's committed yet — playtest it, and once it feels right we'll commit like we did for Level 2.

> AGENT

Done — success now carries you forward, and failure still only offers R. The changes, level by level:

**Level 1 → 2:** After you run the final `entire checkpoint list`, the tutorial now says "something just woke up — press Esc to close the terminal, then **Enter** to investigate." Enter crossfades you straight into the Drone Bay briefing. The walk-around prompt matches ("Enter to investigate · B for orbit"), so B → orbit is still there if you want the scenic route — the glitching pin path still works too, since `level1Done` is set either way.

**Level 2 → 3:** The DISPATCH SENT win screen now reads "press **Enter** to board the ship · B for orbit," and Enter takes you directly into the Launch Clearance briefing. Same deal — orbit is optional, not required.

**Level 3:** unchanged — liftoff is the ending, so B to orbit is the only exit.

**Failure paths:** untouched everywhere. The fail screens accept only R; there was never a path where failing advanced you, so nothing to fix there — the asymmetry you wanted (win moves forward, loss doesn't) was really about wins not moving forward on their own, and now they do.

Build compiles clean. Give the full run a playtest — L1 win → Enter → L2 win → Enter → L3 → liftoff — and if the flow feels right, I'll commit and push it.

  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "  // Stars for the climb out — invisible until the sky turns to space.",
  "new_string": "  // Fireworks for the climb out — gold and lavender bursts the island sends\n  // up past the window. A small recycled pool of Points clouds.\n  const bursts = Array.from({ length: 6 }, () => {\n    const n = 70;\n    const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n    geo.setAttribute(\"position\", new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(n * 3), 3));\n    const mat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n      color: 0xffd27a, size: 1.7, transparent: true, opacity: 0,\n      blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, fog: false,\n    });\n    const pts = new THREE.Points(geo, mat);\n    pts.visible = false;\n    scene.add(pts);\n    return { pts, vel: new Float32Array(n * 3), t: 0, dur: 1.7, active: false };\n  });\n  function fireBurst(b) {\n    b.active = true;\n    b.t = 0;\n    b.pts.material.color.setHex(Math.random() < 0.55 ? 0xffd27a : 0xcdbcff);\n    // Scatter around / below the window so they whoosh past as the ship climbs.\n    const cx = rig.position.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 140;\n    const cy = rig.position.y - 8 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60;\n    const cz = rig.position.z - 50 - Math.random() * 90;\n    const pos = b.pts.geometry.attributes.position.array;\n    for (let i = 0; i < pos.length; i += 3) {\n      pos[i] = cx; pos[i + 1] = cy; pos[i + 2] = cz;\n      const v = new THREE.Vector3().randomDirection().multiplyScalar(9 + Math.random() * 15);\n      b.vel[i] = v.x; b.vel[i + 1] = v.y; b.vel[i + 2] = v.z;\n    }\n    b.pts.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;\n    b.pts.visible = true;\n  }\n  function updateBursts(dt) {\n    for (const b of bursts) {\n      if (!b.active) continue;\n      b.t += dt;\n      if (b.t >= b.dur) { b.active = false; b.pts.visible = false; continue; }\n      const pos = b.pts.geometry.attributes.position.array;\n      for (let i = 0; i < pos.length; i += 3) {\n        pos[i] += b.vel[i] * dt;\n        pos[i + 1] += (b.vel[i + 1] -= 5 * dt) * dt;\n        pos[i + 2] += b.vel[i + 2] * dt;\n      }\n      b.pts.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;\n      b.pts.material.opacity = 0.95 * (1 - b.t / b.dur);\n    }\n  }\n  function resetBursts() {\n    for (const b of bursts) { b.active = false; b.pts.visible = false; b.pts.material.opacity = 0; }\n  }\n\n  // Stars for the climb out — invisible until the sky turns to space."
}

  "new_string": "      // Gold confirm flare: quick bloom, then settles to a steady glow —\n      // and a full-power salute from every beam once the engines are lit.\n      if (s.confirmed) {\n        s.flareT += dt;\n        const settle = 0.28 + Math.sin(t * 2.2 + s.idx) * 0.04;\n        const bloom = Math.max(0, 1 - s.flareT / 2.5) * 0.5;\n        const salute = (igniting || launched) ? 0.45 + Math.sin(t * 7 + s.idx) * 0.15 : 0;\n        s.beam.material.opacity = Math.min(1, settle + bloom + salute);\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (igniting) {\n      // 3… 2… 1… — the rumble builds with the count.\n      ignT += dt;\n      const tick = Math.floor(ignT);\n      if (tick !== ignTick && tick < IGN_DUR) {\n        ignTick = tick;\n        setIgnitionText(String(IGN_DUR - tick), false);\n      }\n      const amp = 0.0015 + (ignT / IGN_DUR) * 0.006;\n      camera.rotation.x = baseRotX + (Math.random() - 0.5) * amp;\n      camera.rotation.y = baseRotY + (Math.random() - 0.5) * amp;\n      if (ignT >= IGN_DUR) igniteNow();\n    } else if (launched && !won) {\n      // Liftoff: the island falls away, the sky becomes space, the island\n      // cheers — fireworks streaming past the window.\n      liftT += dt;\n      const v = Math.min(46, 6 + liftT * 9);\n      rig.position.y += v * dt;\n      const rumble = Math.max(0, 1 - liftT / 3) * 0.006;\n      camera.rotation.x = baseRotX - Math.min(0.5, liftT * 0.07) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * rumble;\n      camera.rotation.y = baseRotY + (Math.random() - 0.5) * rumble;\n      camera.rotation.z = Math.sin(t * 1.3) * 0.004;\n      scene.fog.far = Math.min(2200, 420 + liftT * 320);\n      sun.intensity = Math.max(0.6, 1.5 - liftT * 0.1);\n      burstTimer -= dt;\n      if (burstTimer <= 0 && liftT < LIFT_DUR - 1.5) {\n        const idle = bursts.find((b) => !b.active);\n        if (idle) fireBurst(idle);\n        burstTimer = 0.45 + Math.random() * 0.5;\n      }\n      if (liftT >= LIFT_DUR) {\n        won = true;\n        if (winSub) winSub.textContent =\n          `homeward — ${RECORD_TOTAL} checkpoints · ${QUESTIONS.length} questions · 0 guesses`;\n        winEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n      }\n    } else if (!failed && !won) {\n      // Idle cockpit sway — alive, not nauseating.\n      camera.rotation.x = baseRotX + Math.sin(t * 0.7) * 0.006;\n      camera.rotation.y = baseRotY + Math.sin(t * 0.45) * 0.008;\n    }\n    updateBursts(dt);\n  }"
}

> AGENT

The liftoff is now a proper celebration. Here's the new sequence after the flight log files:

1. **The salute** — all five of your upgrades fire their gold beams at full power, pulsing, the moment the log is filed.
2. **3… 2… 1…** — the console slides away and giant gold numbers pop center-screen, one per second, each with a scale-pop animation, while the cockpit rumble builds underneath you (camera shake that grows with the count).
3. **IGNITION** — the word slams in, a bright gold flash washes over the whole screen, and the climb starts with a heavy rumble that smooths out as you gain speed.
4. **Fireworks** — the island sends you off: gold and lavender particle bursts (a recycled pool of six, so it's cheap) keep firing below and around the window as it falls away, whooshing past as you climb into the stars.
5. The win screen's stat line now opens with **"homeward"** — `homeward — 8 checkpoints · 5 questions · 0 guesses`.

Input is locked during the countdown and climb so nothing can interrupt the moment, and everything resets cleanly on R / exit. Build compiles, module loads in Node.

Tuning knobs if you want to adjust the drama: `IGN_DUR` (countdown length, 3s), `LIFT_DUR` (climb length, 9s), and the `burstTimer` interval (~every half-second) in `launchView.js`.

Give it a playtest — if the send-off lands, I'll commit and push both this and the level-progression flow together.

1	# Planetfall
2	
3	Planetfall is a 3D browser game about a stranded astronaut whose ship AI has
4	lost its memory. To get home, the player restores scattered ship memories while
5	learning the real [Entire](https://docs.entire.io) checkpoint workflow:
6	
7	```text
8	git add -> git commit -> link the commit to an Entire checkpoint
9	```
10	
11	The current build is Level 1, **First Memories**. It teaches the difference
12	between a commit and a checkpoint through a simple game loop: recover a memory,
13	freeze it in ice with `git commit`, then bank it into the ship's memory by
14	accepting Entire's checkpoint link.
15	
16	## Run
17	
18	```bash
19	npm install
20	npm run dev
21	```
22	
23	Open <http://localhost:5173>.
24	
25	Useful commands:
26	
27	```bash
28	npm run build
29	npm run dev -- --host 127.0.0.1
30	```
31	
32	Development shortcuts: <http://localhost:5173/?view=island> jumps straight to
33	Level 1, while <http://localhost:5173/?view=level2> opens the Level 2 dev
34	staging state with Level 1 checkpoints preloaded.
35	
36	## How To Play
37	
38	Orbit view:
39	
40	- Drag to orbit the planet.
41	- Scroll to zoom.
42	- Click the island pin to land.
43	
44	Island view:
45	
46	- Click to capture the mouse.
47	- Move with WASD or the arrow keys.
48	- Look around with the mouse.
49	- Press `Esc` to release the mouse or close completed terminal output.
50	- Press `B` to return to orbit.
51	
52	Level 1 loop:
53	
54	1. Walk up to a glowing memory.
55	2. The ship terminal opens.
56	3. Type `git add` to stage the recovered memory.
57	4. Type `git commit` to freeze it in ice.
58	5. When Entire offers to link the commit, press `y`.
59	6. Repeat for all three memories.
60	7. Type `entire checkpoint list` before the countdown reaches `0:00`.
61	
62	Only linked checkpoints fill the ship memory meter. A committed memory is frozen,
63	but it is not banked until the checkpoint is linked. If time runs out, unbanked
64	memories melt and the run fails; press `R` to try again.
65	
66	## Current Features
67	
68	- Procedural lavender ocean planet with metallic gold islands, atmosphere,
69	  clouds, stars, and a Saturn-style ring.
70	- Orbit-to-island flow with a tracked landing pin and crossfade transition.
71	- Walkable first-person island terrain with terrain-following movement.
72	- Diegetic terminal that accepts the real workflow commands: `git add`,
73	  `git commit`, checkpoint link confirmation, and `entire checkpoint list`.
74	- Ice-block commit metaphor: memories freeze when committed and glow once
75	  checkpointed.
76	- Level-wide countdown, urgent/critical clock states, panic sky shift, fail
77	  screen, and retry.
78	- Checkpoint record cards and in-world labels that show the real
79	  `Entire-Checkpoint: <id>` trailer shape.
80	- Local background music with play, mute, and volume controls.
81	
82	## How It's Built
83	
84	- **Three.js + Vite** power the app.
85	- `index.html` defines the canvas, HUD, terminal, briefing, countdown, fail
86	  screen, checkpoint card, loader, and audio controls.
87	- `src/main.js` owns the renderer, audio controls, view switching, resizing, and
88	  render loop.
89	- `src/planetView.js` builds the orbit scene: planet, clouds, atmosphere, ring,
90	  stars, controls, and landable island pin.
91	- `src/islandView.js` builds Level 1: terrain scene, memory artifacts, terminal
92	  state machine, countdown, checkpoint logic, failure, and reset.
93	- `src/terrain.js` creates the procedural island heightmap, water, and
94	  `heightAt(x, z)` sampler.
95	- `src/firstPerson.js` wraps `PointerLockControls` for WASD/arrow-key walking
96	  glued to the island terrain.
97	- `src/debris.js` defines the memory artifacts and small prop builders reused by
98	  the island level.
99	- `src/planet.js`, `src/noise.js`, `src/atmosphere.js`, and `src/ring.js`
100	  generate the procedural planet visuals.
101	
102	Most visual assets are generated at runtime; the music is bundled locally in
103	`public/audio/asteroid-circuit.mp3`.
104	
105	## Project History
106	
107	This repo started as `signalkit`, a Python telemetry CLI. On June 5, 2026 it
108	pivoted into Planetfall: a procedural Three.js game for teaching Entire through
109	play. The old code and the design evolution are recoverable through git history
110	and Entire checkpoints.
111	
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/planetfall-seed-signalkit/README.md",
  "content": "# Planetfall\n\nPlanetfall is a 3D browser game about a stranded astronaut whose ship AI has\nlost its memory. To get home, the player restores the ship's memory while\nlearning the real [Entire](https://docs.entire.io) checkpoint workflow — three\nlevels, one arc:\n\n1. **First Memories** — bank your own work: commit it, then link the\n   checkpoint. Freeze keeps it; checkpoint banks it.\n2. **The Drone Bay** — five subagents repair the ship in parallel while you\n   can only be in one place. `entire checkpoint explain` shows you what each\n   one did; `entire dispatch` turns the day into a report.\n3. **Launch Clearance** — the finale. From the pilot's chair, the ship's AI\n   asks questions only the record can answer. Pick the tool — a command, or a\n   skill that runs it for you — confirm the answers, complete the launch code,\n   and lift off.\n\nThe thread through all three: work you didn't watch is never a mystery if it\nwas checkpointed. You weren't there — your checkpoints were.\n\n## Run\n\n```bash\nnpm install\nnpm run dev\n```\n\nOpen <http://localhost:5173>.\n\nUseful commands:\n\n```bash\nnpm run build\nnpm run dev -- --host 127.0.0.1\n```\n\nDevelopment shortcuts:\n\n- `?view=island` — straight to Level 1.\n- `?view=level2` (or `?level=2`) — Level 2 with Level 1 checkpoints preloaded.\n- `?view=level3` (or `?level=3`) — Level 3, the cockpit finale.\n- `?view=archive` — a shelved search level kept playable for later.\n\n## How To Play\n\nOrbit view:\n\n- Drag to orbit the planet, scroll to zoom, click the island pin to land.\n\nOn the island (Levels 1–2):\n\n- Click to capture the mouse; move with WASD or the arrow keys.\n- `M` toggles a bird's-eye map (you can keep walking), `Esc` releases the\n  mouse or closes finished terminal output, `B` returns to orbit.\n\n**Level 1 — First Memories.** Walk to a glowing memory; the ship terminal\nopens. Type `git add`, then `git commit` to freeze it in ice. When Entire\noffers to link the commit, press `y` — only linked checkpoints fill the ship\nmemory meter. Bank all three, then run `entire checkpoint list` before the\ncountdown hits `0:00`.\n\n**Level 2 — The Drone Bay.** Five systems are dark and the clock can't be\nbeaten alone. Press `E` at a broken system and a subagent takes the job while\nyou move on — running all five at once is the whole point. When a beam turns\nice-blue, walk up: `entire checkpoint explain` comes pre-filled; press `Enter`,\nread what the subagent actually did, then ADD TO SHIP (`Y`). After the fifth\naccept, type `entire dispatch` to file the day's report.\n\n**Level 3 — Launch Clearance.** No walking — you're in the pilot's chair, and\nthe launch computer won't arm on work nobody can account for. The ship asks\nfive questions about work you never watched. Pick a way to look each one up\n(`1`–`3`): a raw command, a skill that runs it for you, or — occasionally — a\ndead end that costs only seconds. The answer lands on screen; confirm it to\nlock a launch-code segment. Five segments, then `entire dispatch` files the\nflight log: ignition, fireworks, liftoff.\n\nWinning a level offers `Enter` to carry you straight into the next. Failing\nonly ever offers `R` to retry — the clock is the only enemy.\n\n## Current Features\n\n- Procedural lavender ocean planet with metallic gold islands, atmosphere,\n  clouds, stars, and a Saturn-style ring.\n- Orbit-to-island flow with a tracked landing pin and crossfade transitions;\n  wins chain levels directly with `Enter`.\n- Walkable first-person island terrain, plus a bird's-eye map toggle.\n- Diegetic terminals that accept the real workflow: `git add`, `git commit`,\n  checkpoint linking, `entire checkpoint list` / `explain` / `search`, and\n  `entire dispatch` — pre-filled where typing ids would be friction.\n- Five worker subagents that improvise upgrades in parallel (a bent dish comes\n  back as a signal spire), each job sealed under a checkpoint to review.\n- A cockpit finale: rotating tool menus (commands and skills as different\n  lenses on the same record), answer confirms, a filling launch code, and a\n  3-2-1 ignition with fireworks on the climb out.\n- Ice-block commit metaphor, checkpoint record cards, and in-world\n  `Entire-Checkpoint: <id>` trailer labels matching the real shape.\n- One countdown per level with urgent/critical clock states and a panic sky;\n  fail screens with instant retry.\n- Local background music with play, mute, and volume controls.\n\n## How It's Built\n\n- **Three.js + Vite** power the app.\n- `index.html` defines the canvas, per-level HUD blocks, terminals, briefings,\n  countdowns, win/fail screens, loader, and audio controls.\n- `src/main.js` owns the renderer, view switching and level progression,\n  resizing, and the render loop.\n- `src/planetView.js` builds the orbit scene: planet, clouds, atmosphere,\n  ring, stars, controls, and the landable island pin.\n- `src/islandView.js` is Level 1: terrain, memory artifacts, the terminal\n  state machine, countdown, checkpoint logic, failure, and reset.\n- `src/droneBayView.js` is Level 2: five broken systems, five subagents,\n  pre-filled explain reviews, and the dispatch finish line.\n- `src/launchView.js` is Level 3: the cockpit rig, question/tool/answer\n  console, launch code, and the liftoff sequence.\n- `src/archiveView.js` is the shelved search level, kept playable.\n- `src/overhead.js` adds the bird's-eye map; `src/firstPerson.js` wraps\n  `PointerLockControls` for terrain-glued walking.\n- `src/memoryProps.js` holds the shared beam/ice/checkpoint-id props;\n  `src/debris.js` defines the memory artifacts.\n- `src/terrain.js`, `src/planet.js`, `src/noise.js`, `src/atmosphere.js`, and\n  `src/ring.js` generate the procedural terrain and planet visuals.\n\nMost visual assets are generated at runtime; the music is bundled locally in\n`public/audio/asteroid-circuit.mp3`.\n\n## Project History\n\nThis repo started as `signalkit`, a Python telemetry CLI. On June 5, 2026 it\npivoted into Planetfall: a procedural Three.js game for teaching Entire through\nplay. The old code and the design evolution are recoverable through git history\nand Entire checkpoints.\n"
}
